------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE baixatotal
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE baixatotal
Select TmpTot
 
ThisForm.CalcTotal()

Select TmpTot
Delete All For ValorPend = 0 And ValorAce = 0

Select TmpConta
_RegisConta = 0
If !Eof()
	_RegisConta = Recno()
Endif
Set Order to 
Replace All Baixa With 0

Select xPar
_RecnoxPar = 0
If !Eof()
	_RecnoxPar = Recno()
Endif
Replace all vpags with 0

Select TmpBaixa
Dele all for !autos
_Autos = .f.
Scan for Autos
	_Autos = .t.
	Exit
Endscan
ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Pagina.Pendencias.BaixaAut.Value = _Autos

Select TmpConta
Set Order to CrVencs
Scan For Flag
	_RecTmpConta = Recno()
	_Dopecs = Dopes
	_Numecs = Numes
	_Grupocs = Grupos
	_Contacs = Contas
	_Nopercs = Nopers
	_Empcs = Emps
	_Tipocs = Tipos
	mpend	= Moedas
	cotforma = Cotacaos
	vlpend	= Acerto - BaixaAut
	If vlpend <= 0
		loop
	Endif
	Set Order to DbVencs
	Scan for Moedas = mpend And Flag && Primeiro baixa na moeda igual
		Do Case
			Case Baixa + BaixaAut = Acerto
				VlUsado = 0

			Case Baixa + BaixaAut + VlPend > Acerto
				VlUsado = Acerto - Baixa - BaixaAut

			Case Baixa + BaixaAut + VlPend <= Acerto
				VlUsado = VlPend

		EndCase
		Replace Baixa With Baixa + VlUsado 

		If VlUsado > 0
			Insert Into TmpBaixa ( Empos, Nopers, Tipos, Valos, Moedas, Cotacaos, Grupos, Contas,;
									Dopecs, Numecs, Grupocs, Contacs, Moecs, Nopercs, Tipocs, Valocs, Cotacaocs, Empcs);
						Values ( TmpConta.Emps, TmpConta.Nopers, TmpConta.Tipos,;
								VlUsado, mPend, cotforma, TmpConta.Grupos, TmpConta.Contas,;
								_Dopecs, _Numecs, _Grupocs, _Contacs, mPend, _Nopercs, _Tipocs, VlUsado, cotforma, _Empcs)

			VlPend = VlPend - VlUsado

			Select TmpTot
			If !Seek( mPend )
				Insert Into TmpTot ( Moeda ) Values ( mPend )
			Endif
			Replace ValorAce With ValorAce + VlUsado
		Endif
	Endscan

	If VlPend > 0  && Sobrou dinheiro, procura em outras moedas
		Select TmpConta 
		Scan for Moedas # mPend And Flag
			cottotal = TmpConta.Cotacaos
			VlConv = VlPend / cottotal * cotforma
			Do Case
				Case Baixa + BaixaAut = Acerto
					VlUsado = 0

				Case Baixa + BaixaAut + Round( VlConv, 4 ) > Acerto
					VlUsado = Acerto - Baixa - BaixaAut

				Case Baixa + BaixaAut + Round( VlConv, 4 ) <= Acerto
					VlUsado = Round( VlConv, 4 )

			EndCase
			Replace Baixa With Baixa + Round( VlUsado, 4 )

			If VlUsado > 0
				_VlCred = VlUsado / cotforma * cottotal
				Insert Into TmpBaixa ( Empos, Nopers, Tipos, Valos, Moedas, Cotacaos, Grupos, Contas,;
										Dopecs, Numecs, Grupocs, Contacs, Moecs, Nopercs, Tipocs,;
										Valocs, Cotacaocs, Empcs) ;
							Values ( TmpConta.Emps, TmpConta.Nopers, TmpConta.Tipos,;
									VlUsado, TmpConta.Moedas, cottotal, TmpConta.Grupos,;
									TmpConta.Contas, _Dopecs, _Numecs, _Grupocs,;
									_Contacs, mPend, _Nopercs, _Tipocs, _VlCred, cotforma, _Empcs)
				VlConv = VlConv - Round( VlUsado, 4 )

				Select TmpTot
				If !Seek( TmpConta.Moedas )
					Insert Into TmpTot ( Moeda ) Values ( TmpConta.Moedas )
				Endif
				Replace ValorAce With ValorAce + Round( VlUsado, 4 )

				VlPend = VlConv / cotforma * cottotal
			Endif
		Endscan
	Endif

	Select TmpConta
	Set Order to CrVencs
	Go _RecTmpConta

	If VlPend > 0
		Select TmpTot
		If !Seek( TmpConta.Moedas )
			Insert Into TmpTot ( Moeda ) Values ( TmpConta.Moedas )
		Endif
		Replace ValorAce With ValorAce + Round( VlPend, 4 )
	Endif
Endscan

VlPend2	= 0

Select xPar
Set Order to 
Scan
	=ThisForm.PoDatamgr.CursorQuery('SigOpFp','CrFpag','Fpags',xPar.FPags)
	If crFPag.Trocos = 1
		Loop
	Endif
	mpend	= crFPag.Moefpgs
	cotpend = xPar.CotFpgs
	vlpend	= xPar.Valos
	Select TmpConta 
	Set Order to DbVencs
	Scan for Moedas = crFPag.Moefpgs And Flag && Primeiro baixa na moeda igual
		Do Case
			Case Baixa + BaixaAut = Acerto
				VlUsado = 0

			Case Baixa + BaixaAut + VlPend > Acerto
				VlUsado = Acerto - Baixa - BaixaAut

			Case Baixa + BaixaAut + VlPend <= Acerto
				VlUsado = VlPend

		EndCase
		Replace Baixa With Baixa + VlUsado 

		If VlUsado > 0
			Insert Into TmpBaixa ( Empos, Nopers, Tipos, Valos, Moedas, Grupos, Contas,;
									FPags, Bancos, Agencias, NContas, NCheques, Cotacaos) ;
						Values ( TmpConta.Emps, TmpConta.Nopers, TmpConta.Tipos,;
								VlUsado, mPend, TmpConta.Grupos,;
								TmpConta.Contas, xPar.FPags, xPar.Bancos,;
								xPar.Agencias, xPar.Contas, xPar.Numeros, TmpConta.Cotacaos)
			VlPend = VlPend - VlUsado

			Select TmpTot
			If !Seek( mPend )
				Insert Into TmpTot ( Moeda ) Values ( mPend )
			Endif
			Replace ValorAce With ValorAce + VlUsado
			Replace xPar.Vpags with xPar.VPags + VlUsado
		Endif
	Endscan

	If VlPend > 0 && Sobrou dinheiro, procura em outras moedas
		Select TmpConta 
		Scan for Moedas # crFPag.Moefpgs And Flag
			cotforma = cotpend
			cottotal = TmpConta.Cotacaos
			VlConv = VlPend / cottotal * cotforma
			Do Case
				Case Baixa + BaixaAut = Acerto
					VlUsado = 0

				Case Baixa + BaixaAut + Round( VlConv, 4 ) > Acerto
					VlUsado = Acerto - Baixa - BaixaAut

				Case Baixa + BaixaAut + Round( VlConv, 4 ) <= Acerto
					VlUsado = Round( VlConv, 4 )

			EndCase
			Replace Baixa With Baixa + Round( VlUsado, 4 )

			If VlUsado > 0
				Insert Into TmpBaixa ( Empos, Nopers, Tipos, Valos, Moedas, Grupos, Contas,;
										FPags, Bancos, Agencias, NContas, NCheques, Cotacaos) ;
							Values ( TmpConta.Emps, TmpConta.Nopers, TmpConta.Tipos,;
									VlUsado, TmpConta.Moedas, TmpConta.Grupos,;
									TmpConta.Contas, xPar.FPags, xPar.Bancos,;
									xPar.Agencias, xPar.Contas, xPar.Numeros, TmpConta.Cotacaos)

				VlConv2 = VlConv - Round( VlUsado, 2 ) && Tiago - 31/08/2010 - Armazena o valor real da diferença de arredondamento
				VlConv = VlConv - Round( VlUsado, 4 )

				Select TmpTot
				If !Seek( TmpConta.Moedas )
					Insert Into TmpTot ( Moeda ) Values ( TmpConta.Moedas )
				Endif
				Replace ValorAce With ValorAce + Round( VlUsado, 4 )

				VlPend = VlConv / cotforma * cottotal
				VlPend2 = VlPend2 + Iif(Round( VlPend, 2 ) = 0,VlConv2 ,0) && Tiago - 31/08/2010 - Armazena o valor real da diferença de arredondamento				
			Endif
		Endscan

		Select xPar
		Replace xPar.Vpags with xPar.Valos - Round( VlPend, 4 )
	Endif
EndScan
&& Tiago - 31/08/2010 - Armazena na última parcela o valor real da diferença de arredondamento
If VlPend2 <> 0
	Select xPar
	Go bottom
		
	Select TmpConta
	Scan for Moedas # crFPag.Moefpgs And Flag
		cotforma = cotpend
		cottotal = TmpConta.Cotacaos	
		VlConv = VlPend2
		Do Case
			Case Baixa + BaixaAut = Acerto
				VlUsado = 0
			Case Baixa + BaixaAut + Round( VlConv, 4 ) > Acerto
				VlUsado = Acerto - Baixa - BaixaAut
			Case Baixa + BaixaAut + Round( VlConv, 4 ) <= Acerto
				VlUsado = Round( VlConv, 4 )
		EndCase
		Replace Baixa With Baixa + Round( VlUsado, 4 )

		If VlUsado > 0
			Insert Into TmpBaixa ( Empos, Nopers, Tipos, Valos, Moedas, Grupos, Contas,;
									FPags, Bancos, Agencias, NContas, NCheques, Cotacaos) ;
						Values ( TmpConta.Emps, TmpConta.Nopers, TmpConta.Tipos,;
								VlUsado, TmpConta.Moedas, TmpConta.Grupos,;
								TmpConta.Contas, xPar.FPags, xPar.Bancos,;
								xPar.Agencias, xPar.Contas, xPar.Numeros, TmpConta.Cotacaos)
			VlConv = VlConv - Round( VlUsado, 4 )

			Select TmpTot
			If !Seek( TmpConta.Moedas )
				Insert Into TmpTot ( Moeda ) Values ( TmpConta.Moedas )
			Endif
			Replace ValorAce With ValorAce + Round( VlUsado, 4 )

			VlPend2 = VlConv / cotforma * cottotal
		Endif
	EndScan

	VlPend3 = VlPend2 / cotforma * cottotal
	VlPend3 = Iif(VlPend3 < 0,Round( VlPend3, 4 ),0)

	Replace xPar.Vpags with xPar.Valos - Round( VlPend, 4 ) - VlPend3
EndIf


*****************************************
***  TRATAMENTO DO TROCO  ***
*****************************************
Select xPar
Scan
	=ThisForm.PoDatamgr.CursorQuery('SigOpFp','CrFpag','Fpags',xPar.FPags)
	If crFPag.Trocos # 1
		Loop
	EndIf
	Select xPar
	mTroco = xPar.MoeFpgs
	vTroco = xPar.Valos - xPar.VPags
	cTroco = xPar.CotFpgs
	_RecPar = Recno()
	Select xPar
	Scan
		=ThisForm.PoDatamgr.CursorQuery('SigOpFp','CrFpag','Fpags',xPar.FPags)
		Select xpar
		If crFPag.Trocos = 1
			Loop
		Endif
		If xPar.VPags = xPar.Valos
			Loop
		Endif
		If MoeFpgs # mTroco
			VlPend = vTroco * cTroco / xPar.CotFpgs
		Else
			VlPend = vTroco
		Endif

		Do Case
			Case xPar.VPags + Round( VlPend, 2 ) > xPar.Valos
				VlUsado = xPar.Valos - xPar.VPags
			Case xPar.VPags + Round( VlPend, 2 ) <= xPar.Valos
				VlUsado = Round( VlPend, 2 )
		EndCase

		Replace xPar.VPags With xPar.VPags + VlUsado
		VlPend = VlPend - VlUsado
		Select TmpTot
		If !Seek( xPar.MoeFpgs )
			Insert Into TmpTot ( Moeda ) Values ( xPar.MoeFpgs )
		Endif
		Insert Into TmpBaixa ( Empos, Nopers, Tipos, Valos, Moedas, Grupos, Contas, FPags, ;
							   Bancos, Agencias, NContas, NCheques, Cotacaos ) ;
				Values ( crSigCdPgr.Emps, 0, 'Y', VlUsado, xPar.MoeFpgs, PgRecParam.GruAcertos,;
						 PgRecParam.ConAcertos, xPar.FPags, xPar.Bancos, xPar.Agencias,;
						 xPar.Contas, xPar.Numeros, xPar.CotFpgs )
		vTroco = VlPend * xPar.CotFpgs / cTroco
		If vTroco = 0
			Exit
		Endif
	Endscan
	Select xPar
	Go _RecPar
	Replace vPags with Valos - vTroco
Endscan
**********************************************************
***  TRATAMENTO DO CRÉDITO PENDENTE  ***
**********************************************************
Select xPar
Scan
	=ThisForm.PoDatamgr.CursorQuery('SigOpFp','CrFpag','Fpags',xPar.FPags)
	Select xpar
	If crFPag.Trocos = 1
		Loop
	Endif
	If xPar.VPags # xPar.Valos

		mPend = xPar.MoeFpgs
		VlPend = xPar.Valos - xPar.VPags
		Select TmpTot
		If !Seek( mPend )
			Insert Into TmpTot ( Moeda ) Values ( mPend )
		Endif
		Replace ValorAce With ValorAce + VlPend
		If Abs( VlPend * xPar.CotFpgs ) <= PgRecParam.CancCreds
			lcTipoCred = 'Y'
		Else
			lcTipoCred = 'X'
		Endif
		Insert Into TmpBaixa ( Empos, Nopers, Tipos, Valos, Moedas, Grupos, Contas,;
								FPags, Bancos, Agencias, NContas, NCheques, Cotacaos ) ;
					Values ( crSigCdPgr.Emps, 0, lcTipoCred, VlPend, mPend, Spac( 10 ) ,;
							Spac( 10 ), xPar.FPags, xPar.Bancos, xPar.Agencias,;
							xPar.Contas, xPar.Numeros, xPar.CotFpgs )
		Replace xPar.Vpags with xPar.Valos
	Endif
Endscan
**********************************************************
***  TRATAMENTO DO DÉBITO PENDENTE  ***
**********************************************************
Select TmpConta
Set Order to DbVencs
Scan for Flag 
	VlDifere = Acerto - Baixa - BaixaAut
	If VlDifere > 0

		If !Seek( TmpConta.Grupos + TmpConta.Contas + TmpConta.Moedas + ' ', 'TmpBaixa', 'GruConMoe' )
			Insert Into TmpBaixa ( Empos, Nopers, Valos, Moedas, Cotacaos, Grupos, Contas,;
									Grupocs, Contacs, Moecs, Valocs, Cotacaocs);
						Values ( TmpConta.Emps, TmpConta.Nopers,;
								VlDifere, TmpConta.Moedas, TmpConta.Cotacaos,;
								TmpConta.Grupos, TmpConta.Contas,;
								PgRecParam.GruAcertos, PgRecParam.ConAcertos,;
								TmpConta.Moedas, VlDifere, TmpConta.Cotacaos)
		Else
			Select TmpBaixa
			Replace Valos with Valos + VlDifere,;
					Valocs with Valocs + VlDifere
		Endif
	Endif
Endscan

lnTotal = 0
Select TmpTot
Scan
	If !Seek( Moeda, 'TmpCot' )
		m.Moedas = Moeda
		m.Cotacaos = fCarregarCambio( m.Moedas, crSigCdPgr.Datarcs )
		Insert Into TmpCot From Memvar
	Endif
	lnTotal = lnTotal + Round( ( ValorPend - ValorAce ) * TmpCot.Cotacaos, 2 )
Endscan
If !Seek( PgRecParam.Moedaps, 'TmpCot' )
	m.Moedas = PgRecParam.Moedaps
	m.Cotacaos = fCarregarCambio( m.Moedas, crSigCdPgr.Datarcs )
	Insert Into TmpCot From Memvar
Endif
lnTotal = Round( lnTotal / TmpCot.Cotacaos, 2 )
With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Pagina.Pendencias
	.Get_ValConv.Value = lnTotal
	.Get_ValConv.Refresh
Endwith 

Select xPar
Go Top
If _RecnoxPar # 0
	Go _RecnoxPar
Endif

Thisform.mOrdemConta()
If _RegisConta # 0
	Go _RegisConta
Endif

With This.Pagina.Dados.Pagina
	.Pagamento.Total.Refresh
	.Pendencias.Total.Refresh
Endwith
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE buscaliquida
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE buscaliquida
Lparameters lnAgBanco 

Do Case
	Case Inlist(lnAgBanco,1,2) && Bradesco / Itaú
		npos = Ascan(thisform.aLiquida,crRetorno.liquida,1,Alen(thisform.aLiquida,1),1,8)
		cobsliq = Iif(npos>0,'/Liquidado: ' + thisform.aLiquida[npos,2],'')
		npos = Iif(lnAgBanco = 1,Ascan(thisform.aRejeitaBd,Left(crRetorno.erros,2),1,Alen(thisform.aRejeitaBd,1),1,8), ;
					Ascan(thisform.aRejeita,crRetorno.erros,1,Alen(thisform.aRejeita,1),1,8))
		cobsliq = cobsliq + Iif(npos>0,'/ Rejeitado: ' + thisform.aLiquida[npos,2],'')
		m.HistPgs = m.HistPgs + cobsliq					

	Case Inlist(lnAgBanco,3) && Bando do Brasil
		npos = Ascan(thisform.aLiquida,[BB]+crRetorno.comando,1,Alen(thisform.aLiquida,1),1,8)
		cobsliq = Iif(npos>0,'/Comando: ' + thisform.aLiquida[npos,2],'')
		
		lcBusca = crRetorno.NatRec
		If Inlist(crRetorno.comando,[05],[06],[07],[08],[15],[46])
			lcBusca = [L] + crRetorno.NatRec
		Else
			If Inlist(crRetorno.comando,[02])
				lcBusca = [E] + crRetorno.NatRec
			Else
				If Inlist(crRetorno.comando,[09],[10],[20])
					lcBusca = [B] + crRetorno.NatRec
				Endif
			Endif
		Endif
		
		npos = Ascan(thisform.aRejeitaBb,lcBusca,1,Alen(thisform.aRejeitaBb,1),1,8)
		cobsliq = cobsliq + Iif(npos>0,'/Retorno: ' + thisform.aRejeitaBb[npos,2],'')

	Case Inlist(lnAgBanco,4) && Santander
		npos = Ascan(thisform.aLiquida,[ST]+crRetorno.CODOCOR,1,Alen(thisform.aLiquida,1),1,8)
		cobsliq = Iif(npos>0,'/Ocorrência: ' + thisform.aLiquida[npos,2],'')
		m.HistPgs = m.HistPgs + cobsliq			
		
Endcase

Return cobsliq
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE calctotal
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE calctotal
Select TmpTot
Zap

Select TmpConta
_Order = Order()
If Eof()
	_Recno = -1
Else
	_Recno = Recno()
Endif

Set Order to DbVencs
Scan For Flag
	If !Seek( TmpConta.Moedas, "TmpTot" )
		Insert Into TmpTot ( Moeda ) Values ( TmpConta.Moedas )
	Endif
	
	Replace TmpTot.ValorPend With TmpTot.ValorPend + TmpConta.Acerto,;
			 TmpTot.ValorAce With TmpTot.ValorAce + TmpConta.BaixaAut
Endscan

Select TmpConta
Set Order to &_Order
If _Recno > 0
	Go _Recno
Endif

Return
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE calculasaldo
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE calculasaldo
Local Ret
Ret = fRecalculaS( .t., ThisForm.Podatamgr )
Return Ret

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE canbaixaaut
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE canbaixaaut
LParameters _Volta

Select TmpConta
If !Eof()
	_Recno = Recno()
Else	
	_Recno = -1
Endif
Set Order to
Wait Window 'Cancelando Baixa Automática... Aguarde...' Nowait
Scan
	If BaixaAut # 0
		Replace Acerto with Acerto - BaixaAut
		Replace BaixaAut with 0
		If Acerto = 0
			Replace Flag with .f.
		Else
			Replace Flag with .t.
		Endif
	Endif
Endscan
Select TmpBaixa
Delete all for Autos

Thisform.mOrdemConta()
If _Recno = -1
	Go Bott
Else
	Go _Recno
Endif

ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Pagina.Pendencias.Refresh
Wait Clear

ThisForm.BaixaTotal()
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE contacorrenteop
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE contacorrenteop
LParameters llvoper
*** Mena 09/10/2016 - copiado do sigmvcab e adapatado ***
Local ldDtContab, lcRclis, lcGrupoCob, lcContaCob, _pData, lnCont, _lcOcorSv, _lcNota, _lcObs, lcContaPar, ldDtEmis, lcRclis, lnCont
LOCAL _GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, _GrupoTx, ContaTx, _Titulo, _doc, _departamento, _Numc, _Dopc, _hist, _Noper, _Voper
LOCAL _Opers,_SOpers, lcEDNcs

Wait Window 'Aguarde. Processando Lancamentos de Conta Corrente!!! ' Nowait

_pData = Datetime() &&ThisForm.poDataMgr.GetDatetime()
_lcNota = thisform.pagina.dados.pagina.pendencias.get_nota.Value	&& 09/10/2016
_lcObs  = NVL(crsigcdpgr.obs,[])
lcContaPar=RECNO('xpar')
lcRclis = ''
lnCont  = 0
lnRetVOper = 0
_Departamento = Space(10)
_numc = 0 
_dopc = '' 
_LancaCC = .f.
lnvTx    = 0
ldDtEmis = xPar.Datas

xoper = iif(crSigCdOpe.copers=1,'D','C')
xsoper = iif(crSigCdOpe.copers=1,'C','D')

_dopc = 'CONTAS A RECEBER VIII'
_numc = fGerUniqueKey(_dopc)

lcEDNcs = crSigCdPgr.Emps + _dopc + Str(_numc,6)
If Empty(ThisForm.NumeraTitulo)
	ThisForm.NumeraTitulo = ThisForm.GeraNrTitulo(_lcNota, [])
EndIf
_Titulo       = ThisForm.NumeraTitulo &&TmpEest.Notas && Richard em 11/12/2012


ldDtContab = xPar.Vencs

=ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigOpFp','crOpFp','Fpags',xPar.Fpags)
_doc = Transf( crSigCdOpe.NDopes,"@L 999" ) + Transf( crSigcdpgr.Numes, "@L 999999" )

lcTitBan      = Iif(crOpFp.Infos = 'B' And crOpFp.ImpBols = 1,xPar.nCarnes,'')
lnvTx  = Round( xpar.Valos * crOpFp.Desc2s / 100 , 2 ) * crSigMvPar.Cotfpgs		&& mena 06/11/2017 - foi trocado o campo lnValor por xpar.Valos (lnValor estava com o valor total)

If Not IsEmpty(crOpFp.Grupods) And Not IsEmpty(crOpFp.Contads) and crOpFp.ValPends =1 and crOpFp.OriDes = 4

	_GrupoOrig = crSigcdpgr.Grupos
	_ContaOrig = crSigcdpgr.Contas

	
	If Not IsEmpty(_ContaOrig) And ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdCli','CrtmpCli','Iclis',_ContaOrig, [rclis])
		lcRclis = crtmpCli.Rclis
	EndIf

	_Hist = crSigcdpgr.Emps+' '+'RECEBIMENTO ' + ALLTRIM(STR(crSigcdpgr.numes))+' '+xpar.fpags+' '+lcRclis

	_noper = fGerUniqueKey( 'SigMvCcr' )
	If llVOper
		_voper = crSigcdpgr.Emps + Alltrim( Str( lnRetVOper, 9 ) )
	Else
		_voper = crSigcdpgr.Emps + _TIPOE + Alltrim( str( _noper, 9 ) )
	EndIf

	lnValor = Abs( xpar.Valos )

	_Hist2 = Alltrim( crSigCdOpe.Hist2s )

	DO Case
		Case crSigCdOpe.tpHist2s = 1
			_hist2 = _hist2 + ' ' + _Titulo

		Case crSigCdOpe.tpHist2s = 2
			If crOpFp.Infos = [C]
				_Hist2 = _Hist2 + ' Ch:'+xpar.Bancos+'-'+xpar.Agencias+'-'+xpar.Contas+'-'+xpar.numeros
			Else
				If crOpFp.Infos = [T]
					_Hist2 = _Hist2 + ' Ct:'+xPar.cnnsuparcs
				EndIf
			EndIf

		Case crSigCdOpe.tpHist2s = 3
			_hist2 = _hist2 + ' ' + Alltrim(CrTmpCli.Rclis)

		Case crSigCdOpe.tpHist2s = 4
			_hist2 = _hist2 + ' ' + Alltrim(_ContaOrig)

		Case crSigCdOpe.TpHist2s = 5
			_hist2 = _hist2 + ' ' + Iif(!IsNull(_lcobs),Alltrim(_lcobs),'')
	EndCase

	If (crOpFp.TpHists = 4)
		Do Case
			Case (crOpFp.Infos = [C]) && Cheque
				_Hist = _lcnota + [ CHQ: ] + xPar.Bancos + [/] + xPar.Agencias + [/] + ;
						xPar.Contas + [/] + xPar.Numeros
				_Hist2 = [CPF: ] + xPar.Cpfs		

			Case (crOpFp.Infos = [D]) && Duplicata
				_Hist = _lcnota + [ (] + Alltrim(Str(xPar.Parcs, 2)) + [/] + ;
						Alltrim(Str(xPar.TParcs, 2)) + [)]
				_Hist2 = lcRclis
						
			Case (crOpFp.Infos = [O]) && Outros
				_Hist = _lcnota
				_Hist2 = lcRclis
						
			Case (crOpFp.Infos = [R]) && Conta Corrente
				_Hist = _lcnota
				_Hist2 = lcRclis
						
			Case (crOpFp.Infos = [T]) && Cartão
				_Hist = _lcnota + [ - ] + Alltrim(xPar.Outros) + [ - (] + Alltrim(Str(xPar.Parcs, 2)) + [/] + ;
						Alltrim(Str(xPar.TParcs, 2)) + [)]
				_Hist2 = lcRclis

			Otherwise
				_Hist = _lcnota
				_Hist2 = lcRclis
		EndCase 
	EndIf
	
	_CidChave = Dtos(ldDtContab)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(ldDtContab)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
	
	Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Docus, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
			opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
			sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, Dopes, numes, valpags, Hist2s, CidChaves, ;
			EmpDopNums, Dopcs, NumCs, ValoCurs, nfs, Titulos,GrupEms,ContEms, DtEmis, ContaPgs, Tipos ) ;
		Values ( _NOper, _VOper, _Doc, CrSigcdpgr.emps, ldDtContab, usuar, _pData, xPar.Vencs, ;
				_hist, xoper, xsoper, crOpFp.MoeFpgs, 1, lnValor, .t., ;
				_GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, crSigCdPam.gruporecs, crSigCdPam.Contarecs, ;
				crOpFp.MoeFpgs, 1, lnValor, ;
				CrSigcdpgr.Dopes, CrSigcdpgr.numes, lnValor, _Hist2, _CidChave, ;
				CrSigcdpgr.Emps + CrSigcdpgr.Dopes + Str(CrSigcdpgr.Numes,6), _Dopc, _Numc, -lnvTx , ;
				_lcNota, Alltrim( _Titulo ) + '/' + Alltrim( Str( lcContaPar ) ),;
				 _GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig,ldDtEmis, 5, [C])

	Replace crSigMvccr.gruconmoes with crSigMvccr.grupos + crSigMvccr.contas + crSigMvccr.moedas,;
			crSigMvccr.EmpDopncs  With lcEDNcs,;
			crSigMvccr.Pagos      With Iif(crSigMvccr.Valors=crSigMvccr.valpags,'0','1') ,;
			crSigMvccr.DopoTps    with crSigMvccr.Dopcs + crSigMvccr.Opers + crSigMvccr.Tipos + crSigMvccr.Pagos in crSigMvccr
			
	=fRecalculaS( crSigMvCcr.Grupos, crSigMvCcr.Contas, crSigMvCcr.Datas, crSigMvCcr.Moedas, ThisForm.Podatamgr  )

	=ThisForm.FazContra()
	Replace	crSigMvccr.EmpDopncs  With lcEDNcs
	Replace crSigMvccr.titulos with Alltrim( _Titulo ) + '/' + Alltrim( Str( lcContaPar ) )
	replace	crSigMvccr.Dopcs	with _Dopc
	replace	crSigMvccr.NumCs	with _Numc
	replace	crSigMvccr.GrupEms	with _GrupoOrig
	replace	crSigMvccr.ContEms	with _ContaOrig
	replace	crSigMvccr.contapgs	with 0
	=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas, ThisForm.Podatamgr  )

	Select crSigMvPar

	If Not InList(crOpFp.OriDes,1,2)
		Replace NOpers With _NOper, DtAlts With _pData
	EndIf
	***
ENDIF

	*** lança titulo do valor - taxa 
If  crOpFp.Infos = [T] And Not Empty(crOpFp.GrupoDs) And Not Empty(crOpFp.ContaDs) And crOpFp.Desc2s > 0	&& And (_Numc > 0)

	_noper = fGerUniqueKey( 'SigMvCcr' )
	If llVOper
		_voper = crSigcdpgr.Emps + Alltrim( Str( lnRetVOper, 9 ) )
	Else
		_voper = crSigcdpgr.Emps + _TIPOE + Alltrim( str( _noper, 9 ) )
	ENDIF
	
	_GrupoDest = crOpFp.Grupods
	_ContaDest = crOpFp.Contads

	_Opers  = IIF(ThisForm.Tipo = "C", "D", "C")
	_SOpers = ThisForm.Tipo


	pCidc  = Dtos(ldDtContab)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(ldDtContab)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 


*******
	Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Docus, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
			opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
			sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, Dopes, numes, valpags, Hist2s, CidChaves, ;
			EmpDopNums, Dopcs, NumCs, ValoCurs, Nfs, Titulos, GrupEms, ContEms, DtEmis, ContaPgs, Tipos ) ;
		Values ( _NOper, _VOper, _Doc, CrSigCdPgr.Emps, ldDtContab, usuar, _pData, xPar.Vencs, ;
				_hist, xsoper, xoper, crOpFp.MoeFpgs, 1, lnValor, .t., ;
				_GrupoDest, _ContaDest, crSigCdPam.gruporecs, crSigCdPam.Contarecs, ;
				crOpFp.MoeFpgs, 1, lnValor, ;
				CrSigCdPgr.Dopes, CrSigCdPgr.Numes, Abs(lnvTx) , _Hist2, pCidc, ;
				CrSigCdPgr.Emps+CrSigCdPgr.Dopes+Str(CrSigCdPgr.Numes,6), _Dopc, _Numc, -lnvTx , ;
				_lcNota, Alltrim( _Titulo ) + '/' + Alltrim( Str( lcContaPar ) ), ;
				_GrupoDest, _ContaDest,ldDtEmis, 5, [])

	Replace crSigMvCcr.gruconmoes with crSigMvCcr.grupos + crSigMvCcr.contas + crSigMvCcr.moedas,;
			crSigMvCcr.EmpDopncs  With lcEDNcs,;
			crSigMvCcr.Pagos      With Iif(crSigMvCcr.Valors=crSigMvCcr.valpags,'0','1') ,;
			crSigMvCcr.DopoTps    with crSigMvCcr.Dopcs + crSigMvCcr.Opers + crSigMvCcr.Tipos + crSigMvCcr.Pagos in crSigMvCcr
			
	=fRecalculaS( crSigMvCcr.Grupos, crSigMvCcr.Contas, crSigMvCcr.Datas, crSigMvCcr.Moedas, ThisForm.Podatamgr  )
	
	=ThisForm.FazContra()
	Replace	crSigMvccr.EmpDopncs  With lcEDNcs
	replace crSigMvCcr.ValOcurs	  with -lnvTx in crSigMvCcr
	replace crSigMvCcr.titulos	  with Alltrim( _Titulo ) + '/' + Alltrim( Str( lcContaPar ) ) in crSigMvCcr
	replace crSigMvCcr.dopcs	  with _Dopc in crSigMvCcr
	replace crSigMvCcr.numcs	  with _numc in crSigMvCcr
	replace	crSigMvccr.GrupEms	with _GrupoDest
	replace	crSigMvccr.ContEms	with _ContaDest
	replace	crSigMvccr.contapgs	with 0
ENDIF

*******
*!*		Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, Vopers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists, Hist2s, ;
*!*				sopers, opers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
*!*				sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, dopes, numes, valpags, ;
*!*				Cidchaves, GruConMoes, EmpDopNums ) ;
*!*			Values ( _noper, _Voper, crSigCdPgr.emps, ldDtContab, usuar, _pData, xPar.Vencs, ;
*!*					_hist, _Hist2, _sopers, _opers, csSigOpFp.MoeFpgs, 1, Abs( xPar.Valos ), .t., ;
*!*					_GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, _GrupoDest, _ContaDest,;
*!*					csSigOpFp.MoeFPgs, xPar.CotFpgs, Abs( xPar.Valos ), crSigCdPgr.dopes, crSigCdPgr.numes, Abs( lnVtx ),;
*!*					pCidc, _GrupoOrig + _ContaOrig + csSigOpFp.MoeFpgs, ;
*!*					CrSigCdPgr.Emps+CrSigCdPgr.Dopes+Str(CrSigCdPgr.Numes,6))
*!*					
*!*		replace crSigMvccr.ValoCurs	with -lnVtx
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.Nfs	    with _lcNota
*!*		Replace crSigMvccr.titulos  with Alltrim( _Titulo ) + '/' + Alltrim( Str( lcContaPar ) )
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.GrupEms	with _GrupoOrig
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.ContEms	with _ContaOrig
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.DtEmis	with ldDtEmis
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.contapgs	with 5
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.Dopcs	with _Dopc
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.NumCs	with _Numc
*!*		replace crSigMvccr.EmpDopNums	with CrSigcdpgr.Emps + CrSigcdpgr.Dopes + Str(CrSigcdpgr.Numes,6)
*!*		replace crSigMvccr.Dopcs	with _Dopc
*!*		replace crSigMvccr.NumCs	with _Numc
*!*		replace crSigMvccr.EmpDopncs  With crSigMvccr.Emps + crSigMvccr.Dopcs + Str(crSigMvccr.Numcs,6)

*!*		=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas, ThisForm.Podatamgr  )

*!*		ThisForm.FazContra

*!*		replace crSigMvccr.ValoCurs	with -lnVtx
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.Nfs	    with _lcNota
*!*		Replace crSigMvccr.titulos  with Alltrim( _Titulo ) + '/' + Alltrim( Str( lcContaPar ) )
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.GrupEms	with _GrupoOrig
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.ContEms	with _ContaOrig
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.DtEmis	with ldDtEmis
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.contapgs	with 5
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.Dopcs	with _Dopc
*!*		replace	crSigMvccr.NumCs	with _Numc
*!*		replace crSigMvccr.EmpDopNums	with CrSigcdpgr.Emps + CrSigcdpgr.Dopes + Str(CrSigcdpgr.Numes,6)
*!*		replace crSigMvccr.Dopcs	with _Dopc
*!*		replace crSigMvccr.NumCs	with _Numc
*!*		replace crSigMvccr.EmpDopncs  With crSigMvccr.Emps + crSigMvccr.Dopcs + Str(crSigMvccr.Numcs,6)

*!*		=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas, ThisForm.Podatamgr  )


* Taxas de Cartão de Crédito
_GrupoTx = crOpFp.GrupoDs
_ContaTx = crOpFp.ContaDs

If  Not Empty(_GrupoTx) And Not Empty(_ContaTx) and crOpFp.Infos = [T] And Not Empty(crOpFp.GrupoTs) And Not Empty(crOpFp.ContaTs) And crOpFp.Desc2s > 0	&& And (_Numc > 0)


	lcSql = [Select * From SIGOPOCO Where Grupos = ']+crOpFp.GrupoTs+[' And Contas = ']+crOpFp.ContaTs+[' And Opers = 'CR']
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CrSIGOPOCO')
	
	Select CrSIGOPOCO
	If Reccount('CrSIGOPOCO') = 0
		MessageBox('Ocorrencia não Cadastrada para Taxa de Cartão da Condição de Pagamento '+crOpFp.Fpags,0+16,'')
		If llvOper
			Return -1
		Else
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	EndIf
	xsOper = CrSIGOPOCO.Opers
	xOper  = Iif(xsOper=[DB],[CR],[DB])
	
		
	_noper = fGerUniqueKey( 'SigMvCcr' )

	Insert Into crSigOpCoc (Emps,Dopcs,Dataos,Notitulos,Moedas,Cotacaos, EmpDopNcs,CidChaves,Nopers,Valors,Opers,;
							Codigos,ValConvs, Autos ) Values ;
						   (CrSigcdpgr.Emps,_Dopc,Ttod(ThisForm.Datapadrao),_Numc,crSigMvPar.MoeFpgs, crSigMvPar.CotFpgs,;
						   CrSigcdpgr.Emps + _Dopc + Str(_Numc,6), fUniqueIds(), _nOper, lnvTx, CrSIGOPOCO.Opers, ;
						   CrSIGOPOCO.Codigos, Abs(lnvTx), .t.)

	_Hist = Alltrim(crSIGOPOCO.Descrs) + [-] + Alltrim(crSigOpCoc.codigos) + [-] + _Titulo

****
	For lnCont=1 to 2

		lcGOri = Iif(lnCont=1,crOpFp.GrupoTs,_GrupoTx)
		lcCOri = Iif(lnCont=1,crOpFp.ContaTs,_ContaTx)
		lcGDes = Iif(lnCont=1,_GrupoTx,crOpFp.GrupoTs)
		lcCDes = Iif(lnCont=1,_ContaTx,crOpFp.ContaTs)
		lcodc  = Iif(lnCont=1,xOper,xsoper)
		lcSdc  = Iif(lnCont=1,xsoper,xoper)
		lcGPem = ' '
		lcCPem = ' '
			
		If llVOper
			_voper = CrSigcdpgr.Emps + Alltrim( str( _noper, 9 ) )
		Else
			_voper = CrSigcdpgr.Emps + _TIPOE + Alltrim( str( _noper, 9 ) )
		EndIf
		
		If lnvTx = 0
			Loop
		EndIf
		
		_CidChave = Dtos(ldDtContab)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(ldDtContab)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
		lcGrupoCob = ''
		lcContaCob = ''


		Insert Into crSigmvccr ( NOpers, VOpers, Docus, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
				opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, sgrupos, scontas, ;
				smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, Dopes, numes, valpags,	dopcs, numcs, nfs, titulos,;
				grupems, contems, grupages, contages, valliqs, dtemis, Hist2s, CidChaves, EmpDopNums,;
				bContas,TitBans,Tipos) ;
			Values ( _NOper, _VOper, _Doc, CrSigcdpgr.Emps, ldDtContab, usuar, _pData, xPar.Vencs, ;
					_hist, lcodc, lcsdc, crOpFp.MoeFpgs, 1, lnvTx, .t., lcGOri, lcCOri, ;
					lcGDes,lcCDes, crOpFp.MoeFpgs, 1, lnvTx, ;
					CrSigcdpgr.Dopes, CrSigcdpgr.numes, lnvTx ,_dopc, _numc, ;
					_lcNota, Alltrim( _Titulo ) + '/' + Alltrim( Str( lcContaPar ) ),;
					lcGPem, lcCPem, lcGrupoCob, lcContaCob, 0, ldDtEmis, _Hist2, _CidChave, ;
					CrSigcdpgr.Emps + CrSigcdpgr.Dopes + Str(CrSigcdpgr.Numes,6), _Departamento, lcTitBan,'O' )
		Select crSigmvccr
		Replace gruconmoes with grupos + contas + moedas,;
				EmpDopncs  With lcEDNcs,;
				Pagos      With Iif(Valors=valpags,'0','1'),;
				DopoTps    with Dopcs + Opers + Tipos + Pagos
				
*!*				If _Numc > 0
*!*					Replace bGrupos With TmpEest.UsuPagos in crSigMvCcr
*!*				EndIf
			=fRecalculaS( crSigMvCcr.Grupos, crSigMvCcr.Contas, crSigMvCcr.Datas, crSigMvCcr.Moedas, ThisForm.poDataMgr  )
	Next

	Replace VPags    With lnvTx,;
			ValoCurs With lnvTx,;
			Pagos    With Iif(Valos = Vpags, '0', '1') in crSigMvPar
EndIf

****



*!*		For lnCont=1 to 2

*!*			lcGOri = Iif(lnCont=1,crOpFp.GrupoTs,_GrupoTx)
*!*			lcCOri = Iif(lnCont=1,crOpFp.ContaTs,_ContaTx)
*!*			lcGDes = Iif(lnCont=1,_GrupoTx,crOpFp.GrupoTs)
*!*			lcCDes = Iif(lnCont=1,_ContaTx,crOpFp.ContaTs)
*!*			lcodc  = Iif(lnCont=1,xOper,xsoper)
*!*			lcSdc  = Iif(lnCont=1,xsoper,xoper)
*!*			lcGPem = ' '
*!*			lcCPem = ' '
*!*				
*!*			If llVOper
*!*				_voper = CrSigcdpgr.Emps + Alltrim( str( _noper, 7 ) )
*!*			Else
*!*				_voper = CrSigcdpgr.Emps + _TIPOE + Alltrim( str( _noper, 7 ) )
*!*			EndIf
*!*			
*!*			_CidChave = Dtos(ldDtContab)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(ldDtContab)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 

*!*			Insert Into crSigmvccr ( NOpers, VOpers, Docus, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
*!*					opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, sgrupos, scontas, ;
*!*					smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, Dopes, numes, valpags,	dopcs, numcs, nfs, titulos,;
*!*					grupems, contems, valliqs, dtemis, Hist2s, CidChaves, EmpDopNums,;
*!*					bContas,TitBans,Tipos ) ;
*!*				Values ( _NOper, _VOper, _Doc, CrSigcdpgr.emps, ldDtContab, usuar, _pData, xPar.Vencs, ;
*!*						_hist, lcodc, lcsdc, crOpFp.MoeFpgs, 1, lnvTx, .t., lcGOri, lcCOri, ;
*!*						lcGDes,lcCDes, crOpFp.MoeFpgs, 1, lnvTx, ;
*!*						CrSigcdpgr.Dopes, CrSigcdpgr.numes, lnvTx ,_dopc, _numc, ;
*!*						_lcNota, Alltrim( _Titulo ) + '/' + Alltrim( Str( lcContaPar ) ),;
*!*						lcGPem, lcCPem, 0, ldDtEmis, _Hist2, _CidChave, ;
*!*						CrSigcdpgr.Emps + CrSigcdpgr.Dopes + Str(CrSigcdpgr.Numes,6), _Departamento, lcTitBan, 'O')
*!*			Select crSigmvccr
*!*			Replace gruconmoes with grupos + contas + moedas,;
*!*					EmpDopncs  With Emps + Dopcs + Str(Numcs,6),;
*!*					Pagos      With Iif(Valors=valpags,'0','1'),;
*!*					DopoTps    with Dopcs + Opers + Tipos + Pagos
*!*					
*!*			=fRecalculaS( crSigMvCcr.Grupos, crSigMvCcr.Contas, crSigMvCcr.Datas, crSigMvCcr.Moedas, ThisForm.poDataMgr  )

*!*		Next

*!*		Replace VPags    With lnvTx,;
*!*				ValoCurs With lnvTx,;
*!*				Pagos    With Iif(Valos = Vpags, '0', '1') in crSigMvPar

If llvOper 
	Return lnRetVOper
Else
	Return .t.
EndIf

*************** fim ******************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE criacursor
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE criacursor
Select * From CrSigMvSlc Where 1=0 Into Cursor CsTmpSccr ReadWrite

Select *, 0000000.0000 as CotOrigs From CrSigMvPar Where 0=1 Into Cursor xPar ReadWrite
Select xPar
index on vencs tag vencs
index on bancos+agencias+contas+numeros tag chq

Select * From CrSigCdPbx Where 0=1 Into Cursor TmpBaixa ReadWrite
Select TmpBaixa
Index on Grupos + Contas + Moedas + Tipos Tag GruConMoe
Index on Tipos + Str( Nopers, 7 ) Tag BxNoper
Index on Tipocs + Str( Nopercs, 7 ) Tag BxNoperc

Select *, '   ' as MoeComs From CrSigMvCcr Where 0=1 Into Cursor TmpMccr ReadWrite
Select TmpMccr
Index on Grupos+Contas+Hists+Moedas+MoeComs+Hist2s+Opers Tag Comissao2
Index on Grupos+Contas+Dopes+Str(Numes,6)+Moedas+MoeComs+Ttoc(Vencs) Tag Comissao3
Index on Grupos+Contas+Dopes+Str(Numes,6)+Moedas+MoeComs+Opers Tag Comissao

With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Pagina.Pagamento.Grade
	.RecorDSource = 'xPar'
	.fpagto.Controlsource = 'xPar.Fpags'
	.Valor.ControlSource  = 'xPar.Valos'
	.Moeda.ControlSource  = 'xPar.MoeFpgs'
	.Venc.Controlsource   = 'xPar.Vencs'
	.SetAll('DynamicForeColor','iif(xPar.Vencs>CrSigCdPgr.Datarcs,RGB(255,0,0),RGB(0,0,0))','Column')
EndWith

With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Pagina.Pendencias.Grd_Saldo
	.RecorDSource = 'CsTmpSccr'
	.Column1.Controlsource = 'CsTmpSccr.Grupos'
	.Column2.ControlSource  = 'CsTmpSccr.Contas'
	.Column3.ControlSource  = 'CsTmpSccr.Moedas'
	.Column4.Controlsource   = 'CsTmpSccr.Saldos'
EndWith

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE excluir
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE excluir
Local _Numes, _Dopes, _Chave, lcTransacao, _pData

Private ldData

Declare LaCampo(4,2), LaCampo3(2,2), LaCampo4(4,2)

_pData = ThisForm.PodataMgr.GetDatetime()

llDatetime = _pData
_Numes = crSigCdPgr.Numes
_Dopes = crSigCdPgr.Dopes
_Chave = crSigCdPgr.Emps + _Dopes + Str( _Numes, 6 )
lcEDN  = crSigCdPgr.Emps + _Dopes + Str( _Numes, 6 )
llFalse= .f.

lcSql = [Select nFechas From SigCdPgc Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcEdn+[' And nFechas > 0]
ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'TmpPgCli')
Select TmpPgCli
Scan
	lcEdnF = CrSigCdPgr.Emps + Padr('ANTECIPAÇÃO',20) + Str(TmpPgCli.nFechas,6)
	lcUpdate = [Update SigCdPgr Set Concs = ?llFalse Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcEdnF+[']
	ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcUpdate,'')
EndScan

lcDelete = [Delete From SigCdPgc Where EmpDopNums = ?lcEDN ]
lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelete,'')
IF lnErro < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return .f.
Endif

If PgRecParam.ComLancas = 2
	ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPit','CrPrit','EmpDopNums', lcEDN )
	Select CrPrit
	Count to lnMccr
	loBarra = CreateObject( 'fwprogressbar', 'Excluindo baixa de comissões...', lnMccr )
	loBarra.Show
	Scan
		loBarra.Update( .t. )
		_EmpNop = Empos + Str( Nopers, 7 )
		If Tipos = "P"
			laCampo3(1,1)='Nopers'
			laCampo3(1,2)=CrPrit.Nopers
			laCampo3(2,1)='Emps'
			laCampo3(2,2)=CrPrit.EmpOs
			=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvPar','CrPar',@laCampo3)
			lcEDNP = crPar.Emps + crPar.Dopes + Str( crPar.Numes, 6 )
			=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvCab','CrEest','EmpDopNums',lcEDNP )
			=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdOpe','CrOpe','Dopes',CrPar.Dopes )
			_ValBaixa = crPrit.Acertos
			_ValOpera = crEest.Valos
			If crOpe.CMoes # crPrit.Moedas
				_ValBaixa = crPrit.Acertos * crPrit.Cotacaos
				If IsEmpty( crOpe.CMoes )
					_ValOpera = crEest.Valos
				Else
					If !Seek( crOpe.CMoes, 'TmpCot' )
						m.Moedas = crOpe.CMoes
						m.Cotacaos = fCarregarCambio( m.Moedas, crEest.Datas )
						Insert Into TmpCot From Memvar
					Endif
					_ValOpera = crEest.Valos / TmpCot.Cotacaos
					_ValBaixa = crPrit.Acertos / TmpCot.Cotacaos
				Endif
			Endif
			_PercBaixa = _ValBaixa / _ValOpera
			
			ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdCmm','CrComis','EmpDopNums',lcEDNP )
			
			Select crComis
			Scan
				_Baixar = Round( Comissaos * _PercBaixa, 2 )
				If Baixas - _Baixar < 0
					lnBaixa = 0
				Else
					lnBaixa = Baixas - _Baixar
				EndIf
				lcUpDate = [UpDate SigCdCmm Set Baixas = ]+Str(lnBaixa,12,2)+;
						   [ Where CidChaves = ']+CrComis.CidChaves+[']
				LnErro   = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				Endif
			Endscan
		Endif
	Endscan
	loBarra.Complete( .t. )
EndIf

loBarra = CreateObject( 'fwprogressbar', 'Excluindo baixa cruzada de lançamentos de Conta Corrente...', 1 )
loBarra.Show
loBarra.Update( .t. )

lcDelete = [Delete From SigCdPbx Where EmpDopNums = ?lcEDN ]
lnErro = ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcDelete,'')
IF lnErro < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return .f.
Endif

loBarra.Complete( .t. )

ThisForm.Podatamgr.cursorquery('SigCqChm','CrChm','EmpDopNums',lcEDN )

Select CrChm
Scan
	_Cheque =  Bancos + Agencias + NContas + NCheques
	_Lote = NumLotes
	lcDelete = [Delete From SigCqChm Where CidChaves = ']+CrChm.cidChaves+[']
	ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelete,'')
	
	lcWher = [Select * From SigCqChm Where nCheques=']+CrChm.nCheques+[' ]
	lnErro = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrChm2')	
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	
	_Apaga = .f.
	Select * From CrChm2 where Bancos+Agencias+ncontas+Ncheques=_Cheque And EmpDopNums # lcEDN order by DtSis ;
	Into Cursor CrChm3
	Go bott
	If _Tally = 0
		_Apaga = .t.
	Endif

	If ( ThisForm.Tipo = "D" And Codigos = "ENTRADA" ) Or  ( ThisForm.Tipo = "C" And Codigos = "DEPÓSITO" )
		If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvPar','CrPar','Numeros',CrChm.Numeros)
			Select CrPar
			Index On Bancos + agencias + contas + numeros tag cheque
			=Seek(crchm.bancos+crchm.agencias+crchm.contas+crchm.numeros)
			lnValor = 0
			lcpago = '1'
			ldData = _pData
			lcUpdate = [UpDate SigMvPar Set Vpags = ]+Str(lnValor,12,2)+[, pagos = ?lcpago, dtalts = ?ldData Where CidChaves = ']+CrPar.CidChaves+[']
			ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
		EndIf
	EndIf
	
	If _Apaga
		lcDelete = [Delete From SigChe Where Nums =']+CrChm.Nums+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelete,'')
	Else
		lDtAlt = _pData
		lcUpDate = [UpDate SigChe Set Umovs=']+CrChm3.Codigos+[', Grupos =']+CrChm3.Grupos+[', ]+;
					[Contas = ']+CrChm3.Contas+[', DtAlts=?lDtAlt Where Nums=']+CrChm3.Nums+[' ]
		lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
	EndIf
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	Endif
	
	lcDelete = [Delete From SigChLcq Where NumLotes = ]+Str(_Lote)
	lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelete,'')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	Endif

	lcDelete = [Delete From SigCheH Where NumLotes = ]+Str(_Lote)
	lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelete,'')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	Endif

	Select CrChm3
	If !Eof() And !Empty(CrChm3.Numos)
		lcOldEmps    = CrChm3.Emps 
		lcOldNumos   = CrChm3.Numos
		lcOldCodigos = CrChm3.Codigos
		If !Empty(lcOldNumos)
			lcSql  = [Select GruOrigs, ConOrigs From SigChe Where Nums = ']+CrChm3.Nums+[']
			Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CrCheque')
			
			lcWher = [Select * From SigMvCcr Where Emps=']+lcOldEmps+[' And Nopers=]+Str(lcOldNumos)
			ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrMccr')

			Select crMccr
			Go Top
			If Reccount() > 0 And !Empty(CrMccr.Vopers)
				=Seek(lcOldCodigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')
				lcOldVOper = crMccr.VOpers
				lcWher = [Select * From SigMvCcr Where VOpers=']+lcOldVoper+[']
				ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrvMccr')

						lcpago = '1'
						lcDtps = CrvMccr.Dopcs + CrvMccr.Opers + CrvMccr.Tipos + lcPago
						ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute([UpDate SigMvCcr Set ValPags = 0, pagos = ?lcpago, DopoTps = ?lcDtps Where Cidchaves = ']+CrvMccr.CidChaves+['],'')
					Endif
				Endscan
			EndIf
		EndIf
	Endif
EndScan

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE fazbaixaaut
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE fazbaixaaut
LParameters _Volta
Wait Window 'Aguarde... Calculando Baixas Automáticas...' Nowait
Select TmpBaixa
Delete All for Autos
Select TmpConta
Set Order to 
Replace all Acerto With 0, BaixaAut With 0

Set Order to CrVencs
Scan
	_Recno = Recno()
	_Dopecs = Dopes
	_Numecs = Numes
	_Grupocs = Grupos
	_Contacs = Contas
	_Empcs = Emps
	_Nopercs = Nopers
	_Tipocs = Tipos
	_ValorCred = Valos
	_MoedaCred = Moedas
	_CotaCred = Cotacaos

	Set Order to DbVencs
	Scan For Valos # Acerto And _ValorCred # 0

		_CotaConta = Cotacaos
		If Moedas # _MoedaCred
			_ValorCredConv = Round( _ValorCred * _CotaCred / _CotaConta, 4 )
		Else
			_ValorCredConv = _ValorCred
		Endif
	
		If Acerto + _ValorCredConv <= Valos
			Replace Acerto With Acerto + _ValorCredConv,;
					BaixaAut With BaixaAut + _ValorCredConv,;
					Flag With .t.

			Insert Into TmpBaixa ( Empos, Nopers, Tipos, Valos, Moedas, Cotacaos, Grupos, Contas,;
									Dopecs, Numecs, Grupocs, Contacs, Autos, Moecs,;
									Nopercs, Tipocs, Cotacaocs, Valocs, Empcs);
						Values ( TmpConta.Emps, TmpConta.Nopers, TmpConta.Tipos,;
								_ValorCredConv, TmpConta.Moedas, TmpConta.Cotacaos,;
								TmpConta.Grupos, TmpConta.Contas,;
								_Dopecs, _Numecs, _Grupocs, _Contacs, .t., _MoedaCred,;
								_Nopercs, _Tipocs, _CotaCred, _ValorCred, _Empcs)
			_ValorCredConv = 0
		Else
			_Valor = Valos - Acerto
			Replace Acerto With Valos,;
					BaixaAut With Valos,;
					Flag With .t.

			Insert Into TmpBaixa ( Empos, Nopers, Tipos, Valos, Moedas, Cotacaos, Grupos, Contas, Dopecs,;
									Numecs, Grupocs, Contacs, Autos, Moecs, Nopercs, Tipocs, Valocs, Cotacaocs, Empcs);
						Values ( TmpConta.Emps, TmpConta.Nopers, TmpConta.Tipos,;
								_Valor, TmpConta.Moedas, TmpConta.Cotacaos, TmpConta.Grupos, TmpConta.Contas,;
								_Dopecs, _Numecs, _Grupocs, _Contacs,.t., _MoedaCred, _Nopercs, _Tipocs,;
								Round( _Valor  * _CotaConta / _CotaCred, 4 ), _CotaCred, _Empcs)
			_ValorCredConv = _ValorCredConv - _Valor
		Endif
		
		If _ValorCredConv # 0 
			If Moedas # _MoedaCred
				_ValorCred = Round( _ValorCredConv * _CotaConta / _CotaCred, 4 )
			Else
				_ValorCred = _ValorCredConv
			Endif
		Else
			_ValorCred = 0
		Endif
	Endscan
	Set Order to CrVencs
	Go _Recno
	_Valor = Valos - _ValorCred 
	If _Valor > 0
		Replace Acerto With _Valor,;
				BaixaAut With _Valor,;
				Flag With .t.
	Endif
Endscan

ThisForm.BaixaTotal()

Thisform.mOrdemConta()
Go Top

ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Pagina.Pendencias.Refresh
Wait Clear

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE fazcontra
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE fazcontra
Select crSigMvCcr
noper  = crSigMvCcr.nopers
voper  = crSigMvCcr.vopers
oper   = crSigMvCcr.sopers
grupo  = crSigMvCcr.sgrupos
conta  = crSigMvCcr.scontas
moed   = crSigMvCcr.smoedas
cot    = crSigMvCcr.scotacaos
valor  = crSigMvCcr.svalors
soper  = crSigMvCcr.opers
sgrupo = crSigMvCcr.grupos
sconta = crSigMvCcr.contas
smoed  = crSigMvCcr.moedas
scot   = crSigMvCcr.cotacaos
svalor = crSigMvCcr.valors
nlanc  = crSigMvCcr.nlancs
vlanc  = crSigMvCcr.vlancs
data   = crSigMvCcr.datas
dataa  = crSigMvCcr.datalts
valpag = crSigMvCcr.valpags
emp    = crSigMvCcr.emps
hist   = crSigMvCcr.hists
hist2  = crSigMvCcr.hist2s
dope   = crSigMvCcr.dopes
nume   = crSigMvCcr.numes
venc   = crSigMvCcr.vencs
valpag = crSigMvCcr.valpags * scot / cot
contapg= crSigMvCcr.ContaPgs
pCidc  = Dtos(Data)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(data)),"@L 999999") + Thisform.Sigkey 
nf     = CrSigMvCcr.Nfs

Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( VOpers, NOpers, NLancs, VLancs, Opers, Grupos,;
		Contas, Moedas, Cotacaos, Valors, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts,;
		sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, sopers, autos,;
		hists, hist2s, dopes, numes, valpags, vencs, ContaPgs, CidChaves, ;
		GruConMoes, EmpdopNums, Nfs) ;
	Values ( vOper, nOper, nLanc, vLanc, Oper, Grupo, Conta, Moed, Cot,;
		Valor, Emp, Data, Usuar, Dataa, sGrupo, sConta, sMoed, sCot,;
		sValor, sOper, .t., hist, hist2, Dope, Nume, valpag, venc, ContaPg, pCidc,;
		Grupo + Conta + Moed, Emp + Dope + Str(Nume,6), Nf)

=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas, ThisForm.Podatamgr  )

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE geranrtitulo
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE geranrtitulo
Lparameters pNot, pFpg

*** mena - 09/10/2016 - copiado do sigmvcab ***
lcTit = Alltrim(pNot)

If Empty(lcTit) And Not Empty(crSigCdOpe.DopCs)
	If ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigOpFp], [LocalOpFp], [Fpags], pFpg) And (LocalOpFp.ValPends = 1)
		ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigOpOpe], [LocalOpOpe], [Dopes], crSigCdOpe.DopCs, [Identifics])
		lcTit = LocalOpOpe.Identifics + Transform(fGerUniqueKey(crSigCdOpe.DopCs), [@L 999999])
	EndIf
EndIf

Return lcTit
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE impbol
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE impbol
LOCAL lnTpB
lnTpB = 4

*** mena 01/08/2016 - incluidos outros tipos de boleto
=ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigOpCdc','crSigOpCdc','Dopes',crSigCdPgr.Dopes)
if !EMPTY(substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers,233,2))	&& mena - 01/08/2016 - Pega tipo de boleto para Rosana
	lnTpB = VAL(substr(crSigOpCdd.ConfigGers,233,2))
ENDIF
***********************************************************

Do Form SigOpIbo With Thisform, lnTpB,,crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes,0, crSigOpCdc.qvias, crSigOpCdc.qtdbols

*!* mena - 01/08/2016 retirado para poder imprimir os boletos 21,22,23,24,25 Do Form SigOpIbo With Thisform, 4,,crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes,0, crSigOpCdc.qvias, crSigOpCdc.qtdbols

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE imprc
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE imprc
Lparameters _Recibo

=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdOpe','CrOpe','Dopes',CrSigCdPgr.Dopes )

******* impressao boleto
If crSigCdPgr.PgUnicos = 1

	coper = Iif( ThisForm.Tipo = "C", "D", "C" )

	Create Cursor TmpLanca ( Grupos c(10), Contas c(10), Moeda c(3), Cotacaos n(14,4),;
							   GrupoC c(10), ContaC c(10), MoedaC c(3), CotacaoC n(14,4),;
							    Hists c(40), Valor n(12,2), ValPag n(12,2), Tipos c(1), Rclis c(40), ;
							    ValorC n(12,2), ValPagC n(12,2), MoeFpgs c(3), Emps c(3), Vencs d )

	Index on Grupos + Contas + Moeda + Tipos + GrupoC + ContaC + MoedaC Tag GruConMoe
	Select TmpBaixa
	Replace all Grupos With crSigCdPgr.Grupos, Contas With crSigCdPgr.Contas for Tipos = "X"
	Scan
		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigOpFp','CrFpag','Fpags',TmpBaixa.Fpags)
		If crFPag.ValPends = 1 And ThisForm.Tipo = "D"
			Loop
		Endif
		_Grupo = TmpBaixa.Grupos
		_Conta = TmpBaixa.Contas
		_Moeda = TmpBaixa.Moedas
		_Cot = TmpBaixa.Cotacaos
		_Valor = TmpBaixa.Valos
		If IsEmpty( TmpBaixa.Valocs )
			_ValorC = TmpBaixa.Valos
			_CotC = _Cot
			_MoedaC = _Moeda
		Else
			_ValorC = TmpBaixa.Valocs
			_CotC = TmpBaixa.Cotacaocs
			_MoedaC = TmpBaixa.Moecs
		Endif
		If TmpBaixa.Tipos = 'X'
			_Cot = TmpBaixa.Cotacaos
			If ThisForm.MoedaMaior # TmpBaixa.Moedas And !IsEmpty( ThisForm.MoedaMaior )
				If !Seek( ThisForm.MoedaMaior, 'TmpCot' )
					m.Moedas   = ThisForm.MoedaMaior
					m.Cotacaos = fCarregarCambio( m.Moedas, crSigCdPgr.Datarcs )
					Insert Into TmpCot From Memvar
				Endif
				_Cot    = TmpCot.Cotacaos
				_Valor  = Round( TmpBaixa.Valos * _Cot / TmpCot.Cotacaos, 4 )
				_Moeda  = ThisForm.MoedaMaior
				_MoedaC = _Moeda
			Else
				_Valor = TmpBaixa.Valos
			Endif
			_ValPag = 0
		Else
			If TmpBaixa.Tipos = 'Y'
				_Grupo = PgRecParam.GruAcertos
				_Conta = PgRecParam.ConAcertos
			Endif
			_ValPag = _Valor
		Endif

		If crFPag.ValPends = 1 And !( crFpag.Infos = 'C' And COper = 'C' ) And ThisForm.Tipo = 'D'
			Loop
		Endif

		_Tipo  = Iif( TmpBaixa.Tipos # 'Y', 'P', 'Y' )
		If _Tipo = 'Y'
			Loop
		Endif
		If IsEmpty( TmpBaixa.FPags )
			LOOP
		Else
			lcSql = [Select Rclis From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ']+_Conta+[']
			=ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'TmpCli')
			_Rcli = Iif(Used('TmpCli'),TmpCli.Rclis,'')
			
			_hist = crSigCdPgr.Emps + " " + Alltrim( crSigCdPgr.Dopes ) + " " + Alltrim( fGerMascara( crSigCdPgr.Numes ) ) +;
						' ( ' + Alltrim( crFPag.FPags ) + ' )'

			_GrupoC = Iif( coper="C", PgRecParam.gruporecs, PgRecParam.grupopags )
			_ContaC = Iif( coper="C", PgRecParam.contarecs, PgRecParam.contapags )
			Select TmpLanca
			Set Order to GruConMoe
			If !Seek( _Grupo + _Conta + _Moeda + _Tipo + _GrupoC + _ContaC + _MoedaC )
				Insert Into TmpLanca ( Grupos, Contas, Moeda, Hists, Tipos, GrupoC, ContaC,;
										Cotacaos, CotacaoC, MoedaC, MoeFpgs, Emps, Vencs, Rclis ) ;
						Values ( _Grupo, _Conta, _Moeda, _Hist, _Tipo, _GrupoC, _ContaC, _Cot, ;
								_CotC, _MoedaC, xPar.MoeFpgs, crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Datarcs, _Rcli )
			Endif
			Select TmpLanca
			Replace Valor With Valor + _Valor,;
					ValPag With ValPag + _ValPag,;
					ValorC With ValorC + _ValorC
		Endif
	Endscan
	Select TmpLanca
	Scan
		If Valor = 0
			Delete
		Else
			If Abs( TmpLanca.Valor * TmpLanca.Cotacaos ) <= PgRecParam.CancCreds
				DELETE
				LOOP
			Endif
		Endif
	Endscan
	Select TmpLanca
	Go Top
	If _Recibo
		DO Form SigReRcb With ThisForm
	Else
		Do Form SigReIpc with 1
	Endif
Else
	If not InList(crope.docus, 0, 2)
		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigOpCdc','CrDcOPe','Dopes',CrSigCdPgr.dopes)
		lcEDN = crSigCdPgr.Emps + crSigCdPgr.Dopes + Str( crSigCdPgr.Numes, 6 ) 
		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvCab','CrEest','EmpDopNums', lcEDN)
		DO SigPrIdc with crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes, creest.valdevs, crDcOpe.QtdDocs,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,ThisForm
	Endif
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE inchequeserial
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE inchequeserial
If Thisform.ReadComm.Enabled
	thisform.Leitor = .F.
	Thisform.ReadComm.leitura()
	thisform.LeituraSerial=Thisform.readcomm.campo
	Thisform.ReadComm.Enabled = .F.
	KeyBoard "{ENTER}"
	thisform.Pagina.Dados.pagina.pagamento.cheque.get_bco.valid()
	if thisform.leitor
		if !ThisForm.VerifCheq(xpar.bancos,xpar.agencias,xpar.contas,xpar.numeros)
			thisform.Pagina.Dados.pagina.pagamento.cheque.Get_Bco.Value = ''
			thisform.Pagina.Dados.pagina.pagamento.cheque.Get_Age.Value = ''
			thisform.Pagina.Dados.pagina.pagamento.cheque.Get_Num.Value = ''
			thisform.Pagina.Dados.pagina.pagamento.cheque.Get_Conta.Value = ''
			thisform.LeituraSerial = ''
			thisform.Pagina.Dados.pagina.pagamento.cheque.get_bco.setfocus
		else
			thisform.Pagina.Dados.pagina.pagamento.cheque.get_num.setfocus	
		endif		
	else
		thisform.Pagina.Dados.pagina.pagamento.cheque.get_age.setfocus
	endif
	thisform.leitor = .F.
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE inserir
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE inserir
Local lnErro, lcTransacao, llRetorno, _pData, lcVerso, _lcNota

Private ldData

_pData = ThisForm.PodataMgr.GetDatetime()

Go Top In PgRecParam

Declare LaCampo3(2,2), LaCampo(4,2)

COper = Iif( ThisForm.Tipo = "C", "D", "C" )

lnLote  = 99999999
lnNum   = '99999999'
Cidchav = Space(20)

loBarra = CreateObject( 'fwprogressbar', 'Aguarde. Preparando Cursores...', 1 )
loBarra.Show
loBarra.Update( .t. )

Select CrSigCdPgc
Zap
Select CrSigPrTtl
Zap
Select CrSigMvCcr
Zap
Select CrSigCdPit
Zap
Select CrSigMvPar
Zap
Select CrSigChe
Zap
Select CrSigCqChm
Zap
Select CrSigChLcq
Zap
Select CrSigCdCar
Zap
Select CrSigCdCtt
Zap
Select CrSigCdCrd
Zap
Select CrSigCqChi
Zap
Select CrSigCdPbx
Zap
Select CrSigMvCab
Zap
Select CrSigCheh
Zap
Select TmpMccr
Zap

Create Cursor TmpComiss ( Emps c(3), Dopes c(20), Numes n(6), Valos n(12,2), Moedas c(3), Nopers n(6), Cotacaos n(12,4), Contas c(10))
Index on Emps+dopes+Str(Numes,6)+moedas+str(Nopers,9) Tag Empdopnum

Select Grupos, Contas From TmpConta Where Flag Or (Tipos = "M" And Acerto # 0);
Into Cursor TmpCli
Index On Grupos + Contas Tag GruCon

Create Cursor crTmpJuros (Grupos c(10), Contas c(10), Datas t, Moedas c(3), Cotacaos n(16,7), ;
						  Valors n(12,2), TaxJuros n(9,4), Juros n(12,2), Hists c(40), Dias n(10))

loBarra.Complete( .t. )

loBarra = CreateObject( 'fwprogressbar', 'Inserindo lançamentos de Contas...', 1 )
loBarra.Show
loBarra.Update( .t. )

llTrue = .t.
*** Gravando Contas
Select TmpCliente
Scan
	If !Empty( TmpCliente.Grupo ) And !Empty( TmpCliente.Conta ) 
		If TmpCliente.nFechas > 0
			lcEdn = CrSigCdPgr.Emps + Padr('ANTECIPAÇÃO',20) + Str(TmpCliente.nFechas,6)
			lcUpdate = [Update SigCdPgr Set Concs = ?llTrue Where EmpDopNums = ']+lcEdn+[']
			ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcUpdate,'')
		Endif
		If Seek(TmpCliente.Grupo + TmpCliente.Conta,'TmpCli','GruCon')
			Insert Into crSigCdPgc ( Emps, Dopes, Numes, Grupos, Contas, CidChaves, EmpDopNums, nFechas ) ;
					Values ( crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.dopes, crSigCdPgr.numes, ;
							TmpCliente.Grupo, TmpCliente.Conta, fUniqueIds(), ;
							CrSigCdPgr.Emps + CrSigCdPgr.Dopes + Str(CrSigCdPgr.Numes,6), TmpCliente.nFechas )
		Else
			Select TmpCliente
			Delete
		EndIf
	Endif
Endscan
loBarra.Complete( .t. )

loBarra = CreateObject( 'fwprogressbar', 'Inserindo lançamentos de Totais...', 1 )
loBarra.Show
loBarra.Update( .t. )

*** Gravando Totais
Select TmpTot
Scan
	Select TmpCot
	If Seek( TmpTot.Moeda )
		m.Cotacaos = TmpCot.Cotacaos
	Else
		m.Cotacaos = TmpTot.Cotacaos
	EndIf
		
	Insert Into crSigPrTtl ( Emps, Dopes, Numes, Moeda, ValorPend, ValorAce, Cotacaos, CidChaves, EmpDopNums ) ;
				Values ( crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes, TmpTot.Moeda, ;
						TmpTot.ValorPend, TmpTot.ValorAce, m.Cotacaos, fUniqueIds(),;
						CrSigCdPgr.Emps + CrSigCdPgr.Dopes + Str(CrSigCdPgr.Numes,6) )

	*** Calculando se o Recebimento foi a Maior...

	If TmpTot.ValorPend < TmpTot.ValorAce
		_Diferenca = TmpTot.ValorAce - TmpTot.ValorPend
		Select xPar
		Scan for MoeFpgs = TmpTot.Moeda And _Diferenca > 0
			=Seek(xPar.Fpags,'csSigOpFp','Fpags')
			If csSigOpFp.Valpends = 1
				If xPar.VPags - _Diferenca > 0
					Replace xPar.VPags With xPar.VPags - _Diferenca
					_Diferenca = 0
				Else
					_Diferenca = _Diferenca - xPar.VPags
					Replace xPar.VPags With 0
				Endif
			Endif
		Endscan
		If _Diferenca > 0
			Select xPar
			Scan for MoeFpgs = TmpTot.Moeda And _Diferenca > 0
				=Seek(xPar.Fpags,'csSigOpFp','Fpags')
				If CsSigOpFp.Valpends # 1
					If xPar.VPags - _Diferenca > 0
						Replace xPar.VPags With xPar.VPags - _Diferenca
						_Diferenca = 0
					Else
						_Diferenca = _Diferenca - xPar.VPags
						Replace xPar.VPags With 0
					Endif
				Endif
			Endscan
		Endif
	Endif
Endscan
loBarra.Complete( .t. )
loBarra = CreateObject( 'fwprogressbar', 'Inserindo lançamentos de Conta Corrente...', 1 )
loBarra.Show

*** Marcando flag de Conta Baixada na Conta Corrente
Select TmpConta
Set Order to Vencs
Scan For Tipos = 'M' And Acerto # 0

	_Hist = Hists
	_Hist2 = Hist2s
	_Grupo = Grupos
	_Conta = Contas
	_GrupoC = GrupoCs
	_ContaC = ContaCs
	_Valor = Valos
	_Cotacao = Cotacaos
	_Moeda = Moedas
	_ValorC = Valocs
	_CotacaoC = Cotacaocs
	_MoedaC = Moedacs
	_Nf = Nfs
	_Docu = Docus
	
	_noper = fGerUniqueKey( 'SigMvCcr' )
	xoper = TmpConta.Opers
	xsoper = Iif( xOper = 'C', 'D', 'C' )
	pCidc  = Dtos(CrSigCdPgr.Datarcs)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigCdPgr.Datarcs)),"@L 999999") + Thisform.Sigkey 
	Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( Autos, NOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts,;
						Vencs, hists, hist2s, dopes, numes, tipos,;
						opers, grupos, contas, moedas, cotacaos, valors, ;
						sopers, sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors,;
						CidChaves, GruConMoes, EmpDopNums, Nfs, Docus) ;
		Values ( .T., _NOper, crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Datarcs, usuar, _pData,;
				crSigCdPgr.Datarcs, _hist, _Hist2, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes, 'M',;
				xoper, _Grupo, _Conta, _Moeda, _Cotacao, _Valor, ;
				xsoper, _GrupoC, _ContaC, _MoedaC, _CotacaoC, _ValorC, pCidc,;
				_Grupo + _Conta + _Moeda, CrSigCdPgr.Emps + CrSigCdPgr.Dopes + Str(CrSigCdPgr.Numes,6),;
				_nf, _Docu)

	=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas, ThisForm.Podatamgr  )

	ThisForm.FazContra
	
	Select TmpConta
	_OldNoper = NOpers
	Replace NOpers with _Noper
	Select TmpBaixa
	Replace all NOpers with _NOper for Nopers = _OldNoper

EndScan

If Not ThisForm.Podatamgr.Update('CrSigMvCcr')
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return ( .f. )
Endif

Select CrSigMvCcr
Zap

lcVerso = ''
Select TmpLanca
Scan
	If Valor # 0 
		lcSql = [Select Rclis From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ']+TmpLanca.Contas+[']
		If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'TmpCli') < 1
			_Rcli = ''
		Else
			_Rcli = TmpCli.Rclis
		EndIf
		Select TmpLanca
		lcVerso = lcVerso + Padr(Left(_Rcli,39)+' '+Alltrim(Str(Valor,10,2)),50)
	Endif
Endscan

Select TmpConta
Scan For Flag Or (TmpConta.Tipos = "M" And TmpConta.Acerto # 0)
	Insert Into crSigCdPit ( Emps, Dopes, Numes, Empos, Nopers, Tipos, Acertos, Grupos, Contas, ;
				Opers, Cotacaos, Moedas, Hists, Datas, Vencs, Grupocs, Contacs, Valos, ;
				CotUsus, MoedaCs, CotacaoCs, ValoCs, CotOrigs, CidChaves, EmpDopNums, OrdCopChs, Penas ) ;
		Values ( crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes, TmpConta.Emps, TmpConta.Nopers, ;
				TmpConta.Tipos, TmpConta.Acerto, TmpConta.Grupos, TmpConta.Contas, ;
				TmpConta.Opers, TmpConta.Cotacaos, TmpConta.Moedas, TmpConta.Hists, ;
				TmpConta.Datas, TmpConta.Vencs, TmpConta.Grupocs, TmpConta.Contacs, ;
				TmpConta.Valos, TmpConta.CotUsus, TmpConta.MoedaCs, TmpConta.CotacaoCs, ;
				TmpConta.ValoCs, TmpConta.CotOrigs, fUniqueIds(),;
				crSigCdPgr.Emps + crSigCdPgr.Dopes + Str(crSigCdPgr.Numes,6), TmpConta.OrdCopChs, TmpConta.Penas )

	If Flag
		If (TmpConta.Tipos = [P]) Or (ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Pagina.Cadastro.optOperadora.Value = 1)
			lcWher = [Select Emps, Nopers, Valos,Vpags, CidChaves From SigMvPar Where Nopers = ]+Str(TmpConta.Nopers)
			lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrParx')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf
			Select * From CrParx Where Emps = TmpConta.Emps Into Cursor CrPar
			Go Top In CrPar
			lnValor = CrPar.Vpags + TmpConta.Acerto
			lcPago  = Iif(CrPar.Valos<=lnValor,'0','1')
			lddata  = _pData
			lcUpdate = [UpDate SigMvPar Set Vpags = ]+Str(lnValor,12,2)+[, Pagos=?lcpago, dtalts = ?ldData Where CidChaves = ']+CrPar.CidChaves+[']
			lnErro   = ThisForm.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			Endif
		EndIf
		
		*** Tiago - Incluido para atualizar se tem pagamento na operação, conforme a flag SigCdOpe.BxPagos
		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigOpCdc','CrdcOpe','Dopes',tmpconta.dopes)
		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdOpe','CrOpe','Dopes',tmpconta.Dopes)		
		If InList(CrOpe.BxPagos,3,4) Or CrSigOpCdc.BxPagos = 1
			lcEdn = tmpconta.Emps + tmpconta.dopes + Str(tmpconta.numes,6)
			lcSql = [UpDate SigMvCab Set ChkPagos = ?lltrue, UsuPagos =?usuar Where EmpdopNums = ']+lcedn+[']
			lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf		
		EndIf
				
		wErro  = 0

		lcWher = [Select Grupos, Contas, Moedas, Valors, ValPags, CidChaves, Dopcs, Opers, Tipos, ] + ;
						[EmpDopNums, EspecieNfs, Titulos, Hists, Emps, Numcs, EmpDopNcs, Cotacaos, Nopers ] + ;
				   [From SigMvCcr ] + ;
				  [Where Nopers = ] + Str(TmpConta.Nopers,9) + [ And ] + ;
				  		[Emps = ']  + TmpConta.Emps   + [' And ] + ;
				  		[Contas = ']+ TmpConta.Contas + [']

		lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrMccr')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		Endif
		Select crMccr
		If !Eof()
			If ThisForm.Matriz And crSigCdPit.Contas <> crMccr.Contas
				If (crSigCdPit.Grupos # crMccr.Grupos)
					Skip
				Endif
			Else
				If ( crSigCdPit.Grupos + crSigCdPit.Contas # crMccr.Grupos + crMccr.Contas Or crSigCdPit.Moedas # crMccr.Moedas )
					Skip
				Endif
			Endif

			If ThisForm.Matriz And crSigCdPit.Contas <> crMccr.Contas
				lcWher  = [Select Contas From SigMvCcr Where Nopers = ]+Str(crSigCdPit.Nopers,9) + [ And Grupos = '] + crSigCdPit.Grupos + [']
				lnErro  = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'LocalCta')
				If lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return 0
				EndIf
				Go Top In LocalCta
				If Not Eof([LocalCta])
					Replace Contas With LocalCta.Contas In crSigCdPit
				EndIf
			EndIf

			Select crMccr
			If crSigCdPit.Grupos + crSigCdPit.Contas + crSigCdPit.Moedas = Grupos + Contas + Moedas And !Eof()
				Select crMccr
				lnValor = CrMccr.ValPags + TmpConta.Acerto
				lcpago = Iif(CrMccr.Valors<=lnValor,'0','1')
				lnValOc = Iif(CrMccr.Valors<=lnValor,Abs(CrMccr.Valors - lnValor),0)			
				lcDtps = CrMccr.Dopcs + CrMccr.Opers + CrMccr.Tipos + lcPago
*!*					lcUpdate = [Update SigMvCcr Set Valpags = ]+Str(lnValor,12,2)+[, pagos=?lcpago, ] +;
*!*								[DopoTps = ?lcDtps, DataTrans = Null Where CidChaves = ']+CrMccr.CidChaves+[']
*!*					lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
*!*					IF lnErro < 1
*!*						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
*!*						Return .f.
*!*					EndIf

				*** Tiago - Se valor pago > valor título, será lançado uma ocorrência
				lnTotOcud = 0
				lnTotOcuc = 0
				lnTotOcu = 0				
				If CrMccr.Valors < lnValor
					lcOper = Iif(CrMccr.Valors>lnValor,'CR','DB')
					lcquery = [Select top 1 codigos, Grupos, Contas From SigOpOco where opers = ?lcoper] 
					lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcquery,'TmpSigOpOco')
					IF lnErro < 1
						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
						Return .f.
					EndIf

					_noper = fGerUniqueKey( 'SigMvCcr' )
																	
					Insert Into crSigOpCoc (Emps,Dopcs,Dataos,Notitulos,Moedas,Cotacaos, EmpDopNcs, CidChaves, codigos, opers, nopers, valors, valconvs) values ;
										   (crMccr.Emps,crMccr.Dopcs,Date(),crMccr.Numcs,crMccr.Moedas,crMccr.Cotacaos, ;
										    CrMccr.EmpDopNcs, fUniqueIds(), TmpSigOpOco.codigos, lcOper, _noper,lnvaloc,lnvaloc)

					_Hist = TmpConta.Hists
					_Hist2 = TmpConta.Hist2s
					_Grupo = TmpConta.Grupos
					_Conta = TmpConta.Contas
					_GrupoC = TmpSigOpOco.Grupos
					_ContaC = TmpSigOpOco.Contas
					_Valor = lnValOc
					_Cotacao = TmpConta.Cotacaos
					_Moeda = TmpConta.Moedas
					_ValorC = lnValOc
					_CotacaoC = TmpConta.Cotacaos
					_MoedaC = TmpConta.Moedas
					_Nf = TmpConta.Nfs
					_Docu = TmpConta.Docus
					_VOper = _Empr + Alltrim( Str( _NOper, 9 ) ) 

					xoper = lcOper
					xsoper = Iif( xOper = 'C', 'D', 'C' )
					pCidc  = Dtos(CrSigCdPgr.Datarcs)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigCdPgr.Datarcs)),"@L 999999") + Thisform.Sigkey 
					Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( Autos, NOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, VOpers, ;
										Vencs, hists, hist2s, dopes, numes, tipos, EmpDopNcs, ;
										opers, grupos, contas, moedas, cotacaos, valors, ValPags, ;
										sopers, sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, ;
										CidChaves, GruConMoes, EmpDopNums, Nfs, Docus) ;
						Values ( .T., _NOper, crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Datarcs, usuar, _pData, _VOper, ;
								crSigCdPgr.Datarcs, _hist, _Hist2, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes, 'O', CrMccr.EmpDopNcs, ;
								xoper, _Grupo, _Conta, _Moeda, _Cotacao, _Valor, _Valor, ;
								xsoper, _GrupoC, _ContaC, _MoedaC, _CotacaoC, _ValorC,;
								pCidc, _Grupo + _Conta + _Moeda, CrSigCdPgr.Emps + CrSigCdPgr.Dopes + Str(CrSigCdPgr.Numes,6), _nf, _Docu)

					=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas, ThisForm.Podatamgr  )

					ThisForm.FazContra
	
					lcQuery = [Select Opers, ValConvs From SIGOPCOC Where EmpDopNcs = '] + crMccr.EmpDopNcs + [']
					IF ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcquery,'TmpOpCoc') < 1
						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão - TmpOpCoc')
						Return .f.
					Endif
					Select TmpOpCoc
					Scan
						lnTotOcud = lnTotOcud + Iif(TmpOpCoc.Opers='DB',TmpOpCoc.ValConvs,0)
						lnTotOcuc = lnTotOcuc + Iif(TmpOpCoc.Opers='CR',TmpOpCoc.ValConvs,0)
					Endscan
					If CrMccr.Opers='D'
						lnTotOcu = lnTotOcud - lnTotOcuc
					Else
						lnTotOcu = lnTotOcuc - lnTotOcud
					Endif
					Use in TmpOpCoc
				EndIf
				*********

				lcUpdate = [Update SigMvCcr Set Valpags = ]+Str(lnValor - (lnTotOcu + lnValOc),12,2)+[, pagos=?lcpago, ValOcurs = ] + Str(lnTotOcu + lnValOc,12,2) + [,] +;
							[DopoTps = ?lcDtps, DataTrans = Null Where CidChaves = ']+CrMccr.CidChaves+[']
				lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf				

				lcSql = [UpDate SigMvCab Set DataTrans = Null Where EmpdopNums = ']+CrMccr.EmpDopNums+[']
				lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf

				lcSql = [Delete From SigMvEtt Where EmpdopNums = ']+CrMccr.EmpDopNums+[']
				lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf
				
				lcQuery = [Select Grupos,Contas,Moedas,Valors,ValPags,CidChaves,Pagos,Nopers From SigMvCcr Where Nopers = ]+Str(TmpConta.Nopers,9)+[ And Emps = ']+TmpConta.Emps+[']
				lnErro  = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CCorrente')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf
				
				Select cCorrente
				Scan
					If Grupos + Contas + Moedas = crSigCdPit.Grupos + crSigCdPit.Contas + crSigCdPit.Moedas
						If .f.
							If cCorrente.ValPags < TmpConta.Acerto 
								MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Erro de Gravação SigMvCcr. Operacao: '+Str(ccorrente.nopers))
								Return .f.
							EndIf
						Endif	
					EndIf
				EndScan

			Else
				wErro = 2
			EndIf
		Else
			wErro = 1
		Endif
		
		If wErro # 0
			lcTransacao = AllTrim(crSigCdPgr.Emps) + '-' + AllTrim(crSigCdPgr.Dopes) + '-' + ;
						  Str(crSigCdPgr.Numes,6) + ' = ' + ;
						  Iif(wErro = 1, 'Controle: ' + TmpConta.Emps + ;
						  Str(TmpConta.Nopers, 7) + ' Não Encontrado Em SigMvCcr', ;
						  AllTrim(crSigCdPit.Grupos) + '/' + AllTrim(crSigCdPit.Contas) + '/' + ;
						  StrTran(crSigCdPit.Moedas,' ','?') + ' Não Coincide')
			fGravarLog( 'X', Thisform.Name, ThisForm.pcEscolha, lcTransacao )
		Endif
	Endif
	Select TmpConta
Endscan

loBarra.Complete( .t. )
loBarra = CreateObject( 'fwprogressbar', 'Inserindo lançamentos de Cheques...', 1 )
loBarra.Show
loBarra.Update( .t. )
*** Baixando Cheques que foram usados na operação

Select TmpConta
nrlotchq = .f.
valtotch = 0
Scan for Flag And ThisForm.Tipo = "D" && Recebimento
	_Info = ''
	If Tipos = 'P'
		=Seek(Tmpconta.Fpags,'csSigOpFp','Fpags')
		lcWher = [Select Bancos, Agencias, Contas, Numeros, Valos From SigMvPar Where Nopers = ]+Str(TmpConta.Nopers)
		lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrPar')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Go top in crpar
		_Cheque = crPar.bancos + crPar.agencias + crPar.contas + crPar.numeros
		_Info = CsSigOpFp.infos
		_Valchq = crpar.valos
	Else
		laCampo3(1,1)='Numos'
		laCampo3(1,2)=TmpConta.Nopers
		laCampo3(2,1)='Emps'
		laCampo3(2,2)=TmpConta.Emps
		If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery("SigCqChm","CrChm",@LaCampo3,.f.,[Bancos, Agencias, nContas, nCheques, Nums])
			_Cheque = crChm.bancos + crChm.agencias + crChm.ncontas + crChm.ncheques
			ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigChe','CrCheque','Nums',CrChm.Nums, [Valors])
			_Info = 'C'
			_Valchq = crCheque.Valors
		Endif
	Endif
	If _Info = "C"  and !IsEmpty( _Cheque )
		valtotch = valtotch + _ValChq
		nrlotchq = .t.
	Endif
Endscan
vlote = iif( nrlotchq, fGerUniqueKey('lotech'), 0 )

loBarra.Complete( .t. )
loBarra = CreateObject( 'fwprogressbar', 'Inserindo lançamentos de Forma de Pagamento...', 1 )
loBarra.Show
loBarra.Update( .t. )
*** Inserindo Formas de Pagamento

m.datas = crSigCdPgr.datarcs
Select xPar
nrlotchq = .f.
valtotch = 0
llComisVenc = .f.
Scan
	=Seek(xPar.Fpags,'csSigOpFp','Fpags')
	If csSigOpFp.infos = "C"  and !Empty(xPar.bancos)
		nrlotchq = .t.
		valtotch = valtotch + xPar.Valos
	Endif
	llComisVenc = Iif(llComisVenc,llComisVenc,InList(csSigOpFp.nDtComis, 2, 3)) && Tiago - 26/09/2013 - Checa se alguma condição de pagamento está configurada para lançar a comissão com a data de vencimento da parcela
EndScan

vlote = iif( nrlotchq, fGerUniqueKey('lotech'), 0 )

llInsLote = .F.

* Richard em 29/05/2015 - Para que a opção Amos Marque o Campo NFs com o Número do Movimento
*_lcNota = Iif(CsSigOpFp.OpcSNAs = 1,Str(crSigCdPgr.Numes,6),[ ] )
_lcNota = Iif(csSigOpFp.OpcSNAs = 1, Str(crSigCdPgr.Numes,6), ;
		  Iif(csSigOpFp.OpcSNAs = 2, [], ;
		  Iif(crSigCdPgr.CNotas = 1, Str(crSigCdPgr.Numes,6), [])))

Select xPar
cabec=.t.
Scan for !Empty( fpags )
	_Dep = .t.
	Scatter Memvar memo
	_OperFPag = fGerUniqueKey( 'SigMvCcr' )
	m.Nopers  = _OperFPag
	m.Dopes   = crSigCdPgr.Dopes
	m.Numes  = crSigCdPgr.Numes
	m.Emps    = crSigCdPgr.Emps
	m.CidChaves = fUniqueIds()
	m.EmpDopNums = m.Emps + m.Dopes + Str(m.numes,6)
	m.pagos = Iif(m.valos<=m.vpags,'0','1')
	m.dtalts = _pData
	Insert Into crSigMvPar From Memvar
	
	=Seek(xPar.Fpags,'csSigOpFp','Fpags')
	
	Select xPar
	_GrupoDest = csSigOpFp.Grupods
	_ContaDest = Iif(Empty(csSigOpFp.ContaDs),crSigCdPgr.Contas,csSigOpFp.ContaDs)

	_GrupoOrig = Iif( coper="C", PgRecParam.gruporecs, PgRecParam.grupopags )
	_ContaOrig = Iif( coper="C", PgRecParam.contarecs, PgRecParam.contapags )

	if coper = "C"  && Recebimento de Contas
		Do Case 
			Case csSigOpFp.Infos = 'C'  and !Empty(bancos)  && Cheques
				_num=trans(fGerUniqueKey('busnum'),"@L 99999999")
				Insert Into crSigChe ( Bancos, Agencias, NContas, NCheques, Valors, Vencs, Nums, ;
						Umovs, GrClis, IClis, Autos, Emps, FPags, ValPags, Grupos, Contas, ;
						GruCarts, ConCarts, GruOrigs, ConOrigs, DtAlts, DtEmis, Datas ) ;
					Values ( xPar.Bancos, xPar.Agencias, xPar.Contas, xPar.Numeros, xPar.Valos,;
						xPar.Vencs, _Num, 'ENTRADA', crSigCdPgr.Grupos, crSigCdPgr.Contas, .t.,;
						crSigCdPgr.Emps, csSigOpFp.FPags, xPar.VPags, csSigOpFp.GrupoDs, ;
						csSigOpFp.ContaDs, PgRecParam.GruCarts, PgRecParam.ConCarts, crSigCdPgr.Grupos, ;
						crSigCdPgr.Contas, _pData, _pData, _pData )
				Insert Into crSigCqChm ( DtEnts, DtSis, Codigos, Nums, Tipos, Usuarios, NumLotes, ;
						Emps, Dopes, NumDopes, Autos, GrClis, Iclis, Bancos, Agencias, NContas, ;
						NCheques, Grupos, Contas, GruCarts, ConCarts, GruOrigs, ConOrigs, CidChaves, EmpDopNums ) ;
					Values ( crSigCdPgr.Datarcs, _pdata, 'ENTRADA', _num, 'DB', usuar, vlote,;
						crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes, .t., crSigCdPgr.Grupos,;
						crSigCdPgr.Contas, xPar.Bancos, xpar.Agencias, xPar.Contas, xPar.Numeros, ;
						csSigOpFp.GrupoDs, csSigOpFp.ContaDs, PgRecParam.GruCarts, PgRecParam.ConCarts, ;
						crSigCdPgr.Grupos, crSigCdPgr.Contas, fUniqueIds(), CrSigCdPgr.Emps + CrSigCdPgr.Dopes + Str(CrSigCdPgr.Numes,6) ) 

				If !ThisForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigChLcq','CrChm','NumLotes',Vlote) And !llInsLote

					Insert Into crSigChLcq ( DtEnts, DtSis, Datas, Codigos, Nums, Tipos, Usuarios, ;
							NumLotes, Emps, Dopes, NumDopes, Autos, GrClis, Iclis, Moeds,;
							ValTots, ValConvs, GruCarts, ConCarts, GruOrigs, ConOrigs, EmpDopNums ) ;
						Values ( crSigCdPgr.Datarcs, _pData, _pData, 'ENTRADA', _num, 'DB', usuar, vlote,;
							crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes, .t., crSigCdPgr.Grupos,;
							crSigCdPgr.Contas, csSigOpFp.MoeFpgs, ValTotCh, ValTotCh, ;
							PgRecParam.GruCarts, PgRecParam.ConCarts, _GRUPO, _CONTA, crSigCdPgr.Emps + crSigCdPgr.Dopes + Str(crSigCdPgr.Numes,6) )
					llInsLote  = .t.
				Endif
				If !IsEmpty(xPar.Obs)
					Select crSigCheH
					Append Blank
					Replace Nums  With crSigChe.nums,;
							Datas With crSigCdPgr.Datarcs,;
							Hists With Left(xPar.Obs,76),;
							autos With .t.,;
							NumLotes With vLote,;
							Emps  With _Empr,;
							CidChaves With fUniqueIds()
				Endif

			Case csSigOpFp.infos = "T" and csSigOpFp.orides = 4 and crSigCdPac.reccart=1	&&mena 09/10/2016 - copiado do sigmvcab - Rosana

				thisform.contacorrenteop() 								&& gera pernas de cartão TEF e POS somente se flag

			Case csSigOpFp.infos = "T" and !IsEmpty(xpar.nocreditos)	&& Cartão de Crédito

				nlotec = str(fGerUniqueKey('nlotecs'),10)
				ncart = fGerUniqueKey('ncarts')
				Insert Into crSigCdCar ( Emps, Dopes, Numes, Datas, FPags, NLotecs, CidChaves, EmpDopNums ) ;
						Values ( crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes,;
								crSigCdPgr.Datarcs, xPar.DCarts, NLotec, fUniqueIds(),;
								CrSigCdPgr.Emps + CrSigCdPgr.dopes + Str(CrSigCdPgr.Numes,6) )

				Insert Into crSigCdCtt ( Parcs, Datas, FPags, NLotecs, Cartaos, NoCreditos, Valos,;
								NCarts, Emps, Dopes, Numes, Taxas, Iclis, cidChaves, EmpDopNums ) ;
						Values ( xPar.Parcs, crSigCdPgr.Datarcs, xPar.DCarts, NLotec, ;
								 xPar.Cartaos, xPar.NoCreditos, xPar.Valos, nCart, ;
								 crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes, csSigOpFp.Desc2s,;
								 crSigCdPgr.Contas, fUniqueIds(), crSigCdPgr.emps + crSigCdPgr.dopes + Str(crSigCdPgr.numes,6) )

				If csSigOpFp.carauts='S'
					Insert Into crSigCdCrd ( Parcs, Numes, Dt_Deposit, FPags, NoResumos, Recebidos,;
									Valos, Totres, Vencs, Vr_Recebs, Emps, Dopes, Numes,;
									Cartaos, NoCreditos, Datas, Parcs, Ctccs, Ccons, NmCos,;
									Taxas, NCarts, Notas, NLotecs, Iclis, Cidchaves, EmpDopNums ) ;
						Values ( xPar.Parcs, crSigCdCtt.Numes, xPar.Vencs, crSigCdCtt.fpags, ;
								 crSigCdCtt.nocreditos, xpar.vencs, xpar.valos, xpar.valos, ;
								 xpar.vencs, xpar.valos, crSigCdPgr.emps, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, ;
								 crSigCdPgr.Numes, crSigCdCtt.cartaos, crSigCdCtt.nocreditos, ;
								 crSigCdCtt.datas, crSigCdCtt.parcs, csSigOpFp.GrupoDs,;
								 csSigOpFp.ContaDs, crSigCdCtt.nmcos, crSigCdCtt.taxas, crSigCdCtt.ncarts,;
								 crSigCdCtt.Notas, nlotec, crSigCdPgr.Contas, fUniqueIds(), crSigCdPgr.emps+crSigCdPgr.Dopes+Str(CrSigCdPgr.Numes,6) )
					Select crSigCdCtt
					Replace noresumos  with xpar.nocreditos ;
							vencs      with xpar.vencs ;
							dt_deposit with xpar.vencs ;
							ctccs      with csSigOpFp.grupoDs ;
							ccons      with csSigOpFp.contaDs 
				Endif
				Select crSigMvPar
				Replace ncarts with ncart, dtalts with _pData
		Endcase
	Else && coper = "D"  Pagamento de Contas
		If csSigOpFp.infos = "C"  and !Empty(bancos)
			If csSigOpFp.EmiChqs = 1
				=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCqChi','CrChi','nCheques',xPar.Numeros)
				Select CrChi
				Index On Bancos + Agencias + nContas + nCheques Tag Cheque
				If !Seek(xPar.Bancos + xPar.Agencias + xPar.contas + xpar.numeros)
					_copia = fGerUniqueKey( "COPIA DE CHEQUE" + crSigCdPgr.Emps )
					Replace crSigMvPar.NCopias With _Copia, crSigMvPar.dtalts with _pData
					Insert Into crSigCqChi ( Emps, Dopes, Numes, Datas, Bancos, Agencias, ;
										 NContas, NCheques, Valors, Vencs, ;
										 NCopias, Cancelas, Emitidos, Grupos, Contas, ;
										 CidChaves, EmpdopNums, Versos ) ;
								Values ( crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes, crSigCdPgr.Datarcs,;
										 xPar.Bancos, xPar.Agencias, xPar.Contas, xPar.Numeros, ;
										 xPar.Valos, xPar.Vencs, _copia, .F., .F., csSigOpFp.Grupods, csSigOpFp.Contads,;
										 fUniqueIds(), CrSigCdPgr.emps + crSigCdPgr.dopes + Str(crSigCdPgr.numes,6), lcVerso )
				Endif
				If crChi.Emitidos
					llCancela = .f.
					lcUpdate = [Update SigCqChi Set Cancelas = ?llCancela Where CidChaves = ']+CrChi.CidChaves+[']
					ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
				Endif
			Else
				If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvPar','CrPar','Numeros',xPar.numeros)
					Select CrPar
					Index On Bancos + agencias + contas + numeros Tag Cheque
					=Seek(xPar.Bancos+xPar.Agencias+xPar.contas+xpar.numeros)
					lcpago = '0'
					lddata = _pData
					lcUpdate = [UpDate SigMvPar Set Vpags = Valos, pagos=?lcpago, dtalts = ?ldData Where CidChaves = ']+CrPar.CidChaves+[']
					ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
				EndIf
				lcUltGDest = ' '
				lcUltCDest = ' '
				If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigChe','CrCheque','Ncheques',xPar.numeros)
					Select CrCheque
					Index On Bancos + agencias + ncontas + ncheques Tag Cheque
					=Seek(xpar.bancos+xpar.agencias+xpar.contas+xpar.numeros)
					lcUltGDest = CrCheque.Grupos
					lcUltCDest = CrCheque.Contas
					lcUmov = "DEPÓSITO"
					ldDtalt= _pData
					lcGrp  = CrSigCdPgr.Grupos
					lcCta  = CrSigCdPgr.contas
					lcUpdate = [UpDate SigChe Set Umovs = ?lcUmov, DtAlts = ?ldDtAlt, Grupos = ?lcGrp, Contas = ?lcCta ]+;
								[Where Nums =']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
					ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
				EndIf
				
				=Seek(xpar.Fpags,'csSigOpFp','Fpags')

				If csSigOpFp.Valpends = 1
					_GrupoOrig = _GrupoDest
					_ContaOrig = _ContaDest

									Bancos, Agencias, NContas, NCheques, CidChaves, EmpDopNums, GruOrigs, ConOrigs ) ;

					Insert Into crSigChLcq ( DtEnts, DtSis, Datas, Codigos, Nums, Tipos, Usuarios, NumLotes, ;
										Emps, Dopes, NumDopes, Autos, Grupos, Contas, GrClis, Iclis,;
										Moeds, ValTots, ValConvs, EmpDopNums ) ;
								Values ( crSigCdPgr.Datarcs, _pData, _pData, 'DEPÓSITO', _num, 'CR', usuar, vlote,;
										crSigCdPgr.Emps, crSigCdPgr.Dopes, crSigCdPgr.Numes, .t., crSigCdPgr.Grupos,;
										crSigCdPgr.Contas, crCheque.GrClis, crCheque.Iclis, csSigOpFp.MoeFpgs, ;
										ValTotCh, ValTotCh, crSigCdPgr.Emps + crSigCdPgr.Dopes + Str(crSigCdPgr.Numes,6) )
					lcUmov = CrSigChLcq.Codigos
					llInsLote = .t.
				Else
					lcUmov = CrSigCqChm.Codigos
				Endif
				laCampo(1,1)='Bancos'
				laCampo(2,1)='Agencias'
				laCampo(3,1)='nContas'
				laCampo(4,1)='nCheques'
				laCampo(1,2)=xPar.Bancos
				laCampo(2,2)=xPar.Agencias
				laCampo(3,2)=xPar.Contas

					Into Cursor CrPrBx ReadWrite

					Select CrPrBx
					Index On Bancos + Agencias + nContas Tag Cheque
					If Seek(xPar.Bancos + xPar.AGencias + xPar.Contas)

						_Conta2 = PgRecParam.contapags

						_Conta1 = Iif(csSigOpFp.Valpends=1,crPrbx.Contas,csSigOpFp.Contads)

								alltrim( str( vlote, 8 ) ) + ' ( ' + ;
								Alltrim( Transf( xPar.Valos, '999,999,999.99' ) ) + ' ' + ;
								xPar.MoeFpgs + ' ) '
						=Seek(lcUmov,'CrSigCdOpt','Operacao')
						_Opers = iif(crSigCdOpt.tipos='CR','C','D') 
						_Sopers = iif(crSigCdOpt.tipos='CR','D','C')

							Insert Into TmpCot From Memvar

						Scan For !Deposits And Valos # 0 And Bancos + Agencias + nContas = xPar.Bancos + xPar.AGencias + xPar.Contas

							IF lnErro < 1

								Return .f.
							Endif

						EndScan
					Endif

							laCampo3(1,2)=CrPrbx.Nopers

							laCampo3(2,2)=CrPrBx.Empos

									Insert Into TmpComiss ( Emps, Dopes, Numes, Moedas, Nopers, Cotacaos, Contas ) ;

							laCampo3(2,1)='Emps'

									_ValOpera = crEest.Valos
								Else
*!*										_ValOpera = crEest.Valos / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas ) && Tiago - 13/05/14 - Comentei pois não precisava converter o valor da operação pois ele já está na moeda correta; com isso o cálculo do percentual baixado ficava errado
									_ValBaixa = _ValBaixa / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas )
								Endif
